### [1] [SLVR: Structured Latent Visual Reasoning via Human-like Reasoning Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10563)
*Albert Gao,Bing Xue,Andrea Zanette*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SLVR 将多模态推理组织为规划、定位、证据选择、整合四类隐式阶段，并用对应信号分别监督，在避免文本CoT推理开销的同时提升细粒度视觉推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM 常依赖语言先验而非任务相关视觉证据作答；文本CoT虽能分解为证据搜索步骤，但自回归生成增加推理成本；现有隐式推理又难以控制中间状态编码内容，无法对规划、定位、证据选择分别施加监督。

Method: 提出结构化隐式视觉推理（SLVR）训练框架。先通过屏蔽答案提示文本、将正确答案与视觉合理干扰项对比，迫使模型依赖图像；再将推理组织为规划、定位、证据选择、整合四个类型化隐式阶段，分别用计划、边界框、视觉证据、最终理由进行对应监督，从而在推理时不生成文本链而具备显式功能结构。

Result: 基于 Qwen2.5-VL-7B，在多模态推理基准上稳定提升：MMVP +9.4、BLINK Relation +14.2，并在 V*、MathVista、ChartQA 上也有提升。

Conclusion: 结构化隐式监督可在无文本CoT解码开销的情况下提升细粒度视觉推理，为弥合显式CoT与隐式推理提供可行路径。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) often answer visual reasoning questions by relying on linguistic priors rather than task-relevant visual evidence. Textual chain-of-thought reasoning can partially mitigate this issue by encouraging models to decompose visual questions into intermediate evidence-seeking steps, but generating these steps autoregressively increases inference cost. Latent reasoning avoids explicit rationale generation, but existing approaches provide limited control over what intermediate states encode, making it difficult to impose separate supervision for planning, grounding, and evidence selection. We propose Structured Latent Visual Reasoning (SLVR), a training framework that bridges explicit chain-of-thought and latent reasoning by organizing multimodal reasoning into typed latent stages for planning, grounding, evidence selection, and reasoning integration.
  SLVR first trains the model to rely on the image by masking answer-revealing text and contrasting the correct answer with visually plausible distractors. It then organizes reasoning into latent stages for planning, grounding, evidence selection, and integration, supervising each stage with the corresponding signal: plans, boxes, visual evidence, and final rationales. This gives latent reasoning an explicit functional structure while avoiding generated textual chains at inference time.
  Built on Qwen2.5-VL-7B, SLVR improves consistently across multimodal reasoning benchmarks, with absolute gains of +9.4 on MMVP and +14.2 on BLINK Relation, as well as improvements on V*, MathVista, and ChartQA. These results suggest that structured latent supervision can improve fine-grained visual reasoning without the decoding overhead of textual CoT. Project page is available \href{https://bogao-code.github.io/SLVR/}{here}.

</details>


### [2] [A Camera-Native Stereo VR180 Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10607)
*Linxuan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发布首个自主拍摄的立体VR180数据集（1211个样本），包含相机原生Blackmagic RAW、双眼原生鱼眼HEVC与半等距柱状HEVC三种格式、出厂镜头标定及转换工具；并基于x265重编码实验发现，同等视口质量下原生鱼眼编码的码率需求（中位数+38%）在各视场区域均高于半等距柱状编码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公开VR180研究资源多来自YouTube等平台，已经过未知流程的拼接、投影与压缩，且缺乏镜头标定，难以支撑编解码与视口质量等底层研究，因此需要一手采集、带原生格式与标定的数据集。

Method: 使用两台Blackmagic URSA Cine Immersive相机采集立体VR180内容，共1211个样本（636段立体视频，2220.8秒，多为90 fps；575张立体静态图），每个样本提供相机原生Blackmagic RAW、分眼原生鱼眼HEVC（每眼8160x7200）和半等距柱状HEVC（每眼7200x7200）三种格式，并附出厂镜头标定、便携式鱼眼/半等距柱状转换工具及AI生成场景与视觉挑战标注。随后对24段片段的鱼眼与半等距柱状渲染以x265重编码，覆盖双眼、4个码率点和9个观看方向进行对比。

Result: 在24段片段、双眼、4个码率点、9个观看方向的全部条件下，达到相同视口（viewport）质量时，原生鱼眼编码所需码率均高于半等距柱状编码，中位数高出约38%，且该结论在整个视场范围内一致成立。

Conclusion: 该数据集为VR180研究提供了带标定、多原生格式与转换工具的一手资源；实验结果说明在等视口质量约束下原生鱼眼编码的率失真效率不及半等距柱状编码，为VR180投影与编码方案选择提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Immersive VR180 video is increasingly produced with professional stereo fisheye cameras, yet public VR180 research resources are mostly collected from online platforms such as YouTube: already stitched, projected and compressed by unknown pipelines, and without lens calibration. We present a firsthand-captured stereo VR180 dataset recorded with two Blackmagic URSA Cine Immersive cameras. It contains 1,211 samples -- 636 stereo video clips (2,220.8 s, mostly 90 fps) and 575 stereo stills -- each released as camera-native Blackmagic RAW, separate-eye native fisheye HEVC (8160x7200 per eye) and half-equirectangular HEVC (7200x7200 per eye), together with the factory lens calibration, portable fisheye/half-equirectangular conversion tools and AI-generated scene and visual-challenge annotations. Re-encoding the released fisheye and half-equirectangular renders with x265 over 24 clips, both eyes, four rate points and nine viewing directions, native-fisheye coding needed more bitrate than half-equirectangular coding at equal viewport quality for all 24 clips (median +38%), in every part of the field of view. Data: https://huggingface.co/datasets/lulinxuan/VR180 ; code: https://github.com/lulinxuan/vr180-dataset-tools

</details>


### [3] [Seeing Through the Glare: A Multi-Source Benchmark and Ocular-Adaptive Pixel MeanFlow for Eyeglass Reflection Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10703)
*Tao Liu,Youwei Pang,Kailai Zhou,Jiaming Zuo,Hanqi Liu,Wei Ji,Peng-Tao Jiang,Xiaofeng Liu,Weisi Lin,Xiaoqi Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出眼镜反光去除的基准数据集OcuBench与恢复框架OcuFlow。OcuFlow结合几何自适应表示与单步像素MeanFlow（pMF），并采用原生分辨率保频合成，在多种反光条件下于去除质量、眼部保真度与效率上取得一致优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 眼镜反光去除在智能手机成像、视频会议等以人脸为中心的视觉应用中十分重要，但该任务困难：反光从轻度光度污染到严重眼部遮挡不等，需要选择性校正与合理重建，同时不能改变身份或自然外观。现有数据集覆盖的反光条件有限，限制了模型在复杂真实场景中的泛化与系统评估。

Method: 1）构建OcuBench多源基准，包含10,280对可控合成图像对、732对真实输入伪图像对、458张独立真实世界测试图像，支持配对评估及超出生成监督的评测。2）提出OcuFlow，一种眼部自适应像素MeanFlow（pMF）框架，用于高效、保细节的恢复。3）将几何自适应表示与单步pMF结合，把重建聚焦于被反光遮挡的眼部区域。4）采用原生分辨率保频合成，以保留可靠的观测细节。

Result: 在多种反光条件下的实验表明，OcuFlow在反光去除质量、眼部保真度与效率上均取得一致优势。在盲测用户研究中，其获得67.32%的选择率，为次优方法的6.2倍。

Conclusion: OcuFlow与OcuBench为眼镜反光去除提供了有效的框架与系统的评测基准，代码与数据集将公开释放。

Abstract: Eyeglass reflection removal is important across smartphone imaging, video conferencing, and other face-centric visual applications. The task is challenging because reflections range from mild photometric contamination to severe ocular occlusion, requiring selective correction and plausible reconstruction without altering identity or natural appearance. Existing datasets cover limited reflection conditions, constraining generalization to complex real-world scenes and systematic evaluation. We introduce \textbf{OcuBench}, a multi-source benchmark comprising 10,280 controllable synthetic pairs, 732 real-input pseudo-pairs, and 458 independent real-world test images, supporting both paired evaluation and assessment beyond generated supervision. We further propose \textbf{OcuFlow}, an ocular-adaptive pixel MeanFlow (pMF) framework for efficient, detail-preserving restoration. It combines geometry-adaptive representation with one-step pMF to focus reconstruction on reflection-obscured ocular regions, together with native-resolution frequency-preserving synthesis to retain reliable observed details. Experiments across diverse reflection conditions demonstrate that OcuFlow achieves consistent advantages in reflection removal quality, ocular fidelity, and efficiency. In a blind user study, it receives $67.32\%$ of selections, $6.2\times$ the next-best share. Both the code and dataset will be released.

</details>


### [4] [LinSlot: Exploiting Linear Representation hypothesis for unsupervised attribute discovery from slot based object representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10722)
*Sanket Gandhi,Utkarsh Giri,Varun Subramanium,Rohan Paul,Parag Singla*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于线性表示假设（LRH）的概率框架，联合发现图像中的对象（slot）与属性（block）表示，通过 block attention 连接属性与对象并将 LRH 嵌入两个表示空间，以优化 ELBO，在多个数据集上提升 DCI 指标并支持图像编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从原始非结构化图像中无监督学习对象及属性的解耦表示具有重要价值。现有基于 block-slot attention 的方法假定对象表示向属性的分解是均匀的，这一假设可能次优，从而限制所学表示的质量。

Method: 引入线性表示假设（LRH），即组合性概念可表示为 slot 表示中的线性可加子空间；据此构建连接图像、slot（对象）与 block（属性）的概率模型，并设计架构：用 block attention 将属性表示与 slot 相连，同时在对象与属性两类表示空间中加入 LRH 约束，从而有效优化该图模型的证据下界（ELBO）。

Result: 实验表明：(i) 能有效发现解耦的对象与属性表示；(ii) 为 slot 空间中的 LRH 提供了实证支持；(iii) 由于表示解耦且可解释，可实现图像编辑；在多个数据集上 DCI 分数优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 通过以 LRH 为核心构建概率模型并优化 ELBO，可联合学习对象与属性的解耦表示，避免均匀分解假设的局限，提升表示质量与解耦指标，并支持图像编辑应用。

Abstract: This paper studies the problem of learning disentangled representations of objects and their attributes from raw, unstructured image data. Slot-based methods have shown considerable success in unsupervised learning of object representations from images. Block-slot attention-based methods extend this framework to attribute representations by assuming a uniform factorization of object representations into attributes, which may be suboptimal and consequently limit the quality of the learned representations. We therefore investigate a framework for jointly discovering object and attribute representations. Our key contribution is leveraging the Linear Representation Hypothesis (LRH), which postulates that composable concepts can be represented as linearly additive subspaces in slot representations. Based on this insight, we propose a probabilistic model connecting images, slots (objects), and blocks (attributes). We present an architecture that leverages block attention to connect attribute representations to slots and incorporates LRH in both object and attribute representation spaces. This architecture effectively optimizes the Evidence Lower Bound (ELBO) of the proposed graphical model. Our experiments demonstrate (i) effective discovery of disentangled object and attribute representations, (ii) empirical evidence for LRH in slot space, and (iii) the ability to perform image editing owing to the disentangled and interpretable nature of the learned representations. Our experiments on multiple datasets demonstrate improvements in DCI scores over state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [5] [MESSENGER: Memory-Enhanced Sequential Scene Flow Estimation via Autoregressive Next-Frame Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10759)
*Jiuming Liu,Jianing Li,Mengmeng Liu,Hongyang He,Hesheng Wang,Per Ola Kristensson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出记忆增强的序列场景流估计框架 MESSENGER，通过显式记忆库与不确定性感知重加权模块挖掘长时依赖、抑制误差累积，在 nuScenes 与 Argoverse 2 上实现长时域外推的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期成对估计器仅依赖瞬时双帧运动，缺乏长时时间关联，未来预测外推能力差；近期多帧序列到序列方法则随输入帧数增加导致计算开销剧增，且因运动传播无效造成长时域预测性能退化。

Method: 设计记忆缓冲区，显式存储多段历史流估计与隐状态；对每帧输入，关联并检索时序存储的流与状态，以逐帧预测方式生成当前初始流。并引入不确定性感知重加权模块，过滤不可靠检索结果、缓解误差累积。整体采用自回归预测范式。

Result: 在 nuScenes 与 Argoverse 2 上取得最优性能，长时域未来外推中 EPE3D 分别降低 71.6% 与 67.7%。

Conclusion: 性能优势源于自回归预测范式，迫使网络基于历史观测逐步学习下一帧分布，从而实现长时依赖挖掘与稳健外推。

Abstract: Scene flow can capture low-level 3D motion displacements in dynamic scenarios. Early pairwise estimators relying on instantaneous two-frame motion lack long-term temporal correlation and also struggle with poor extrapolation ability in future prediction. Although some recent methods attempt to explore multi-frame scene flow estimation in a sequence-to-sequence manner, they typically suffer from heavy computational overhead with increasing input frames and long-horizon prediction degradation due to ineffective motion propagation. To address these problems, we propose a novel memory-enhanced sequential scene flow pipeline, called MESSENGER. To sufficiently mine long-term temporal dependencies naturally within consecutive sequences, a memory buffer is designed by explicitly storing multiple history flow estimates and latent states. For each input frame, the temporally stored flows and states are correlated and retrieved to predict the current initialized flow in a next-frame forecasting manner. Furthermore, we develop an uncertainty-aware reweighting module to filter unreliable retrievals and mitigate accumulated errors. Extensive experiments on nuScenes and Argoverse 2 demonstrate state-of-the-art performance of our MESSENGER, reducing EPE3D by 71.6% on nuScenes and 67.7% on Argoverse 2 in long-horizon future extrapolation. This superiority can be attributed to our designed autoregressive forecasting paradigm, which naturally forces the network to progressively learn the next-frame distribution based on history observations. Code will be released at https://github.com/liujiuming123/Messenger.

</details>


### [6] [DOGS: Design-Space Sampling for Prompt-Driven Logo Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10760)
*Ganyu Zou,Chen Dai,Nathan Self,Kevin Piper,Ramachandra Rao Seethiraju,Karthik Shyamsunder,Chang-Tien Lu,Naren Ramakrishnan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出DOGS，将logo提示词优化从文本重写重新表述为结构化设计空间采样，利用GFlowNet采样器生成设计图，在可识别性、美学、多样性和商标侵权规避上优于九个基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生图（T2I）的提示词优化几乎完全作为"文本重写"进行，将简短的用户指令扩展为更长的token序列。作者认为这种语言空间表述不适合logo创建等结构化视觉设计任务：一行简短指令会遗漏大量设计决策，这些决策依赖线性序列无法编码的关系先验，并留下一个不受控的通道，可能复现受保护的标志。因此需要将logo提示重新表述为结构化设计空间中的采样问题。

Method: 作者提出DOGS（Design-space prompting with an Originality-aware GFlowNet Sampler）。首先从大规模真实logo语料中挖掘类型化的、图结构的设计语法，其边记录经验共现关系。随后用GFlowNet采样器生成设计图，采样概率正比于终末奖励，该奖励综合可识别性、美学与语料相对原创性。每个槽位仅从封闭的设计级词汇表中抽取，任何诱发侵权或有害的token在解析阶段被移除。原创性奖励进一步惩罚与现有logo的接近程度，从而在设计上内建了侵权规避机制。

Result: 在两个开源渲染器上、与九个基线方法对比，DOGS生成的logo更具可识别性和美学性，多样性显著更高，且远不易发生商标侵权。

Conclusion: 将logo提示词优化从语言空间重写转向结构化设计空间采样是有效且更合适的表述；通过GFlowNet结合原创性感知奖励与封闭设计词汇表，可在方法层面内建侵权规避，同时提升可识别性、美学和多样性。

Abstract: Prompt optimization for text-to-image (T2I) generation has been pursued almost entirely as text rewriting, in which a short user brief is expanded into a longer, model-preferred token sequence. We argue that such a language-space formulation is ill-suited to structured visual design tasks such as logo creation, where a one-line brief leaves most design decisions unspecified. These decisions depend on relational priors that a linear sequence cannot encode, and they leave an uncontrolled channel through which protected marks may be reproduced. We therefore recast logo prompting as sampling within a structured design space, and instantiate this idea as DOGS (Design-space prompting with an Originality-aware GFlowNet Sampler). From a large corpus of real-world logos, we mine a typed, graph-structured design grammar whose edges record empirical co-occurrence. A GFlowNet sampler then generates design graphs with probability proportional to a terminal reward that combines recognizability, aesthetics, and corpus-relative originality. Every slot draws only from a closed design-level vocabulary, and any infringement-inducing or harmful token is removed during parsing. The originality reward further penalizes proximity to existing logos, thereby incorporating infringement avoidance into the method by construction. On two open-source renderers and against nine baselines, DOGS produces logos that are more recognizable and aesthetic, substantially more diverse, and far less prone to trademark infringement.

</details>


### [7] [MiniVer-V: Identifying Minimal Sufficient Evidence for Short Video Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11233)
*Leran Chen,Lingnan Kong,Zile Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出以"证据充分性"作为短视频事实核查的证据选择准则，构建MiniVer-V基准（195个短视频、5,510个多模态证据单元、三分类判定），并设计两层核查框架结合充分性驱动的贪心搜索，实现以少量证据（约4.5单元，占全集16%）达到与全证据基线相当的Macro-F1，并在证据不足时明确弃权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短视频事实核查的核心挑战在于判断哪些证据足以支撑核查结论。现有方法要么将全部证据交给验证器而引入噪声，要么按主题相关性选择证据，混淆了"相关性"与"充分性"。

Method: 1）提出以"证据充分性"为选择准则，即某证据子集是否足以在无冗余的情况下支撑一个可信判定。2）构建MiniVer-V基准：195个短视频，含支持/反驳/不足三分类标注及5,510个多模态证据单元（视觉关键帧、语音转写、网络检索外部来源）。3）提出两层验证框架，将基于内部证据的"主张—视频一致性"评估与需外部佐证的"事实判定"分离。4）在其上引入充分性驱动的贪心搜索，累积证据直至满足充分性阈值；候选池耗尽时输出"不足"，而非强行给出判定。

Result: 使用Claude Sonnet 4时，方法平均仅用4.5个证据单元（占全集16%）即达到Macro-F1 0.510，与全证据基线（27.7单元、0.518）在统计上无显著差异；同时相比不带弃权的相同搜索，显著提升了对"证据不足"案例的识别。效率结论在GPT-5.5上可复现，在开源Qwen2.5-72B验证器上仅部分成立。消融实验表明，外部证据对事实判定不可或缺，内部视频证据则使判定扎根于主张—视频一致性。

Conclusion: 研究表明证据高效的核查是可行的，且当证据确实不充分时，需要显式的弃权机制。

Abstract: A core challenge in short-video fact-checking is identifying which evidence is sufficient to support a verification conclusion. Existing approaches either give the verifier all available evidence, introducing noise, or select evidence by topical relevance, which conflates relatedness with sufficiency. We identify evidential sufficiency as the selection criterion: whether a subset of evidence is adequate to support a confident verdict without redundancy. We introduce MiniVer-V, a benchmark of 195 short videos with three-way verdict annotations (supported, refuted, insufficient) and 5,510 multimodal evidence units spanning visual keyframes, speech transcripts, and web-retrieved external sources. We propose a two-layer verification framework that separates claim-video consistency, assessed from internal evidence, from factual verdict determination, which additionally requires external corroboration. On top of it, a sufficiency-driven greedy search assembles evidence until a sufficiency threshold is met and outputs insufficient when the candidate pool is exhausted, rather than forcing a verdict. With Claude Sonnet 4, the method reaches a Macro-F1 of 0.510 using 4.5 evidence units on average (16% of the full evidence set), statistically indistinguishable from the full-evidence baseline (0.518 with 27.7 units), while significantly improving recognition of insufficient cases over the same search without abstention. The efficiency result replicates with GPT-5.5 and holds only partially with an open-weight Qwen2.5-72B verifier. Ablations show that external evidence is indispensable for factual determination, while internal video evidence grounds the verdict in claim-video consistency. These findings suggest that evidence-efficient verification is achievable, and that explicit abstention is needed when evidence is genuinely inadequate.

</details>


### [8] [VICO: Visual Environments Co-Evolving for Vision-Language Model Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10782)
*Meng Lu,Ligeng Zhu,Olivia Xiao,Yuchen Zhuang,Zihan Wang,Kuncheng Wu,Bangya Liu,Yu Wang,Charles Fleming,Wenqi Shi,Xuan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VICO，一种让视觉语言模型（actor）与环境改写器（EnvRewriter）共同演化的框架，通过编辑图像侧可验证结构并按通过率校准难度，实现无需人工标注的可扩展RLVR后训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）在视觉语言模型后训练中通常假设训练环境静态不变。随着actor能力提升，固定任务的难度会脱离其学习前沿：部分任务变得过于简单，部分始终无法解决，导致学习信号塌缩。因此作者主张视觉后训练应让视觉环境与actor同步演化，而不仅是演化actor本身。

Method: VICO采用actor与Environment-as-Rewriter（EnvRewriter）联合训练的协同演化框架。EnvRewriter编辑可验证的图像侧结构（如场景图、图表表格、受保护区域掩码），并重新渲染生成标签有效的训练样本；通过基于通过率（pass-rate）的奖励，将样本难度校准到actor当前能力水平。该循环在无需额外人工标注的情况下，持续将任务难度与actor能力重新对齐。

Result: 在覆盖数学推理与视觉定位理解的九个多模态基准上，VICO-8B相比基座模型在域外任务上最高提升+5.0%；分别超过最强自演化基线与文本编辑协同演化基线+4.3%和+8.4%；并在使用少16–160倍标注样本的情况下，与图表专用RLVR方法表现相当。

Conclusion: VICO将监督来源从人工标注转向图像编辑协同演化，为视觉推理提供了一条超越静态语料RLVR的可扩展路径。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become a standard recipe for post-training vision-language models (VLMs),
  but it typically assumes a static training environment. As the actor improves, fixed tasks drift out of its learning frontier: many
  become trivial, others remain unsolvable; and the learning signal collapses. We argue that VLM post-training should evolve the
  visual environment alongside the actor, not just the actor itself. We propose VICO, a co-evolutionary framework in which an actor
  and an Environment-as-Rewriter (EnvRewriter) are trained jointly: the EnvRewriter edits verifiable image-side structures, such as
  scene graphs, chart tables, or protected region masks, and re-renders them to produce label-valid training samples whose difficulty
  is calibrated to the actor's current ability through a pass-rate-based reward. This loop continuously realigns task difficulty with
  actor capability without any additional human annotation. Across nine multimodal benchmarks spanning mathematical reasoning and
  visually grounded understanding, VICO-8B improves over its base model by up to +5.0% on out-of-domain tasks, surpasses the strongest
  self-evolution and text-editing co-evolution baselines by +4.3% and +8.4% respectively, and stays comparable to chart-specialized
  RLVR methods using 16-160 times fewer labeled samples. By shifting from human-labeled supervision to image-editing co-evolution,
  VICO offers a scalable path beyond static-corpus RLVR for visual reasoning.

</details>


### [9] [Open-Vocabulary Audio-Visual Event Localization via Complex-Valued Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11846)
*Anirudh Praveen,Koteswar Rao Jerripothula,Pratik Joshi,Aveen Dayal,Neela Sawant*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出四流复数神经网络框架，用虚部伴随流为视觉/音频提供互补表示，通过学习复数相似度融合替代固定规则，在OV-AVEL任务上刷新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇音视频事件定位方法将视觉-文本与音频-文本两路余弦相似度用固定规则（几何平均、加权平均）压缩为单一标量再取argmax，融合方式僵化，限制了多模态信息利用与未见类泛化能力。

Method: 基于冻结的多模态基础模型（ImageBind）得到视觉帧、音频梅尔谱与类名的共享嵌入；为异常表示补入虚部伴随流——视觉用iHSV虚部，音频用CycleGAN转换的相位谱，构成复数表示；对每段视频计算视觉-文本、音频-文本两个复数相似度，再用复数神经网络（CVNN）学习融合；仅训练时间注意力模块与融合CVNN，编码器冻结。另提出双流替代方案。

Result: OV-AVEBench开放（未见类）划分达66.5/59.1/54.1% Acc/Seg-F1/Event-F1，较此前微调基线提升+1.6/+4.1/+6.6，已见类也一致提升；改造后的AVE数据集达60.7/51.9/50.4%，同样取得SOTA；双流方案亦显著优于基线。

Conclusion: 以复数表示与可学习复数融合替代固定相似度聚合规则，能有效提升开放词汇音视频事件定位的未见类与已见类性能，两个基准均达SOTA，且双流简化变体仍具优势。

Abstract: Open-Vocabulary Audio-Visual Event Localization (OV-AVEL) labels each video segment with an event class, including classes that were never seen during training. The dominant pipeline uses a frozen multimodal foundation model (e.g. ImageBind) to embed the visual frame, the audio mel-spectrogram, and each candidate class name into a shared space, then computes two cosine similarities for each segment against each class: visual-text and audio-text. Existing methods then collapse this pair into a single scalar score with a fixed rule (geometric mean, weighted average) before taking the argmax. Instead, we compute complex-valued similarities and learn their fusion using a complex-valued neural network (CVNN). Each modality's standard representation becomes the real part of our pipeline, and a paired companion stream supplies the imaginary part. We use imaginary part of iHSV for visual modality and CycleGAN-translated phase spectrogram for audio modality as these companion streams. This results in two complex similarities, which are then fused. While the vision and audio encoders remain frozen, only the temporal-attention blocks and the fusion CVNN are trained. The four-stream complex architecture sets a new state of the art on both OV-AVEL benchmarks. On the open (unseen-class) split of OV-AVEBench we reach 66.5/59.1/54.1% Acc/Seg-F1/Event-F1 (+1.6/+4.1/+6.6 over the previously reported fine-tuned baseline), with consistent gains for seen classes as well. We also modify AVE dataset for this task and observe that our architecture reaches 60.7/51.9/50.4% Acc/Seg-F1/Event-F1, achieving state-of-the-art OV-AVEL results on it as well. We also propose a two-stream alternative, which also sees great improvements over the baseline.

</details>


### [10] [Velocity Scaling in Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10823)
*Youssef Saied,François Fleuret*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文否定了"速度场幅值被低估"这一主流解释，指出速度缩放本质上是在校正"群体时间滞后"（population time lag），即模型时间 $t$ 处的采样状态更像更早时刻的训练状态；据此测量并选择增益可显著提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期工作发现将流匹配速度场 $v_θ$ 按增益 $γ(t)$ 缩放能大幅提升生成质量，并认为原因是MSE训练系统性地低估了速度幅值，缩放是在修正该误差。作者要检验这一解释是否成立，并寻找真正的机制。

Method: 首先验证MSE训练是否真的造成速度幅值不足；进而提出替代假设——速度缩放会降低群体时间滞后，并从机制上论证"速度缩放"与"把模型时间往回移"是处理该滞后的两种等价手段；随后跨多种架构与模型规模，测量群体时间滞后并以其为依据选择增益 $γ(t)$。

Result: 发现MSE训练并不导致速度幅值缺失，推翻了原解释；测量群体时间滞后并用于选取增益后，在ImageNet-256、NFE 25、无引导条件下，FID由28.0降至12.2（由相邻增益处FID测量值的线性插值估计），且结论在多种架构与模型规模上一致。

Conclusion: 速度缩放的收益源于校正群体时间滞后而非补偿速度幅值；群体时间滞后是一个可测量、可解释的指标，可作为增益选择的原则性工具，稳定提升生成质量。

Abstract: Scaling a learned flow-matching velocity field $v_θ$ by a gain $γ(t)$ was recently shown to greatly improve generation quality. Prior work argued that velocity fields trained with mean-squared error (MSE) systematically underestimate velocity magnitude and that scaling corrects this error. We show that MSE training does not create a velocity-magnitude deficit. We find instead that velocity scaling reduces population time lag: sampled states at model time $t$ resemble training states from an earlier time. Velocity scaling and moving model time back are two ways to address this population time lag. Across architectures and model sizes, measuring population time lag and using it to select a gain greatly improves generation quality, reducing FID from 28.0 to 12.2 (estimated by linear interpolation between FID measurements at neighboring gains) on ImageNet-256 at NFE 25 without guidance.

</details>


### [11] [VINCIE-NExT: Unlocking Video Editing from Images via In-Context Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12104)
*Leigang Qu,Feng Cheng,Ziyan Yang,Bangbang Yang,Zhaoyang Huang,Wei Chow,Yicong Li,Wenjie Wang,Tat-Seng Chua,Yan Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VINCIE-NExT 通过将视频编辑分解为 Video→Image→Image→Video 的子任务链，利用图像编辑对作为上下文视觉示范，在无需大规模成对视频编辑数据的情况下实现视频编辑，并在 OpenVE-Bench 上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建视频编辑器比视频生成器困难得多：视频编辑需要 (source, instruction, edited) 三元组，这类标注代价高昂且难以规模化合成；而图像编辑领域已相对成熟，拥有数百万可用的成对数据。因此，本文希望将图像编辑能力迁移至视频编辑，以缓解视频编辑配对数据稀缺的问题。

Method: 提出统一框架 VINCIE-NExT，将视频编辑分解为结构化的可组合子任务链（Video → Image → Image → Video），把编辑意图经由图像域路由，从而在统一扩散目标下对异构图像与视频语料进行可扩展联合训练。模型或用户提供的图像编辑对被前置为上下文视觉示范，作为每一输出帧的空间外观蓝图。为此提出一种新颖的位置编码，将图像示范与视频帧链接到共享空间坐标系中，实现外观变化向每一输出帧的像素级忠实传播。此外，Chain-of-Editing 通过在测试时按渐进式扩散阶段执行子任务链，实现有原则的测试时扩展，无需重新训练即可通过增加算力提升编辑质量。

Result: 在 OpenVE-Bench 上的综合实验表明，该方法在多种编辑类别上均达到最先进（state-of-the-art）性能；消融实验进一步验证了各组成部分的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过以图像编辑对作为上下文视觉示范并借助结构化子任务链，VINCIE-NExT 有效规避了对大规模成对视频编辑数据的依赖，实现了高质量的像素级视频编辑，并支持通过测试时算力投入持续提升编辑质量。

Abstract: Building a capable video editor remains significantly harder than a video generator: editing requires (source, instruction, edited) triplets that are prohibitively expensive to annotate and difficult to synthesize at scale, whereas image editing has already reached maturity with millions of such pairs readily available. In this work, we introduce VINCIE-NExT, a unified framework that transfers editing capability from images to videos through in-context visual demonstrations, alleviating the need for large-scale paired video editing data. VINCIE-NExT decomposes video editing into a structured chain of composable sub-tasks (Video -> Image -> Image -> Video), routing editing intent through the image domain and enabling scalable joint training from heterogeneous image and video corpora under a unified diffusion objective. An image editing pair, synthesized by the model or supplied by the user, is prepended as an in-context visual demonstration that serves as a spatial appearance blueprint for every output frame. To ground appearance edits across the interleaved context, we introduce a novel position encoding that links image demonstrations and video frames in a shared spatial coordinate system, enabling pixel-faithful propagation of appearance changes to every output frame. Chain-of-Editing further provides principled test-time scaling: by executing the sub-task chain as progressive diffusion stages, editing quality can be improved by investing additional compute without retraining. Comprehensive experiments on OpenVE-Bench demonstrate the state-of-the-art performance across diverse editing categories, with ablations confirming the effectiveness of each component.

</details>


### [12] [Enabling Preference-driven Unlearning in Few-step Distilled Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10859)
*Gaurav Patel,Jun Fang,Greg Ver Steeg,Qiang Qiu,Sravan Sripada*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种偏好驱动的遗忘框架，通过针对少步生成特性改进的DPO公式，直接在少步蒸馏扩散模型中移除目标概念，同时保持少步效率与非目标能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文生图扩散模型正被蒸馏为少步变体以加速推理，但其可能生成有害或不良内容，存在安全风险。现有数据驱动的遗忘方法依赖多步去噪动力学假设，在少步蒸馏模型上失效；而在非蒸馏基座模型上遗忘后再重蒸馏，计算与时间开销过大，不切实际。

Method: 提出偏好驱动的遗忘框架，重新审视面向扩散模型的直接偏好优化（DPO）。指出标准DPO及其遗忘变体基于噪声预测误差，因生成动力学改变而难以迁移至少步蒸馏模型；为此引入与少步生成特性显式对齐的改进偏好优化公式，实现少步蒸馏模型中的直接概念移除。

Result: 主要在身份与NSFW（裸露）移除任务上评估，并扩展到物体级遗忘。大量实验表明该方法遗忘一致有效、保留性能强。

Conclusion: 该方法在保持少步效率与期望能力保留的同时，实现了对少步蒸馏模型的有效遗忘，是实用且有原则的解决方案。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models are increasingly distilled into few-step variants and being deployed to enable fast inference. However, their ability to generate harmful or undesired content poses significant safety risks. Data-driven unlearning methods suppress targeted generations by fine-tuning model weights using specialized unlearning objectives. Crucially, these objectives implicitly rely on multi-step denoising dynamics, an assumption that breaks down for few-step distilled (FSD) models, resulting in ineffective forgetting. Furthermore, performing unlearning on the non-distilled base model and subsequently re-distilling it to obtain an unlearned FSD model incurs substantial computational and time overhead, making it impractical in many settings. Hence, we address this limitation with a preference-driven unlearning framework that revisits Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) for diffusion models. We show that standard DPO and its unlearning derivatives, formulated around noise-prediction error, transfer poorly to FSD models due to their altered generation dynamics. To overcome this, we introduce a modified preference optimization formulation explicitly aligned with the few-step generation properties, enabling direct concept removal in FSD models while preserving few-step efficiency and maintaining strong retention of desirable (non-targeted) capabilities. We evaluate our framework primarily on identity and NSFW (nudity) removal tasks and also extend our method to object-level unlearning. Extensive experiments demonstrate consistent and effective forgetting, and strong retention performance, establishing our method as a practical and principled solution for unlearning in FSD models.

</details>


### [13] [LVS: Local View Synthesis from Relative Camera Pose by Reusing Previous Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12127)
*Qizhou Huo,Xuan Sun,Yongfei Guo,Zhipeng Wang,Yuanhao Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种逐场景的 RGB-D 图像复用框架，用相对位姿引导的几何变形加多尺度残差网络替代邻近视角的重复渲染，在保持低查询延迟的同时提升画质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式场景探索中视角更新频繁，但小幅度相机运动下可见内容高度重叠；传统 3D Gaussian Splatting 对每个目标视角都完整重渲染，未利用这种图像重叠，导致计算冗余。仅靠几何变形又无法恢复新暴露区域，且对深度误差敏感。

Method: 逐场景框架：利用深度与相对位姿进行几何变形，将已渲染的源图像内容传输到目标视角；用轻量多尺度网络预测 RGB 残差，修正变形伪影并推断缺失外观；同时缓存源特征以减少重复计算。

Result: 在 GS-render 上，残差细化相比纯变形将 PSNR 提升约 0.72 dB；在真实采集场景与渲染场景上的评测均显示较低的查询延迟。

Conclusion: 将场景渲染与局部视角更新解耦，可支撑响应式场景探索，在增强现实与虚拟现实中具有应用潜力。

Abstract: Interactive scene exploration requires frequent view updates, although small camera motions preserve much of the visible content. Conventional 3D Gaussian Splatting nevertheless renders each target view, leaving this image overlap unexploited. Reusing rendered images offers an alternative. Geometric warping alone cannot recover newly exposed content and remains sensitive to depth errors. We propose a per-scene framework that replaces repeated scene rendering for nearby views with relative-pose-guided RGB-D image reuse. Geometric warping uses depth and relative pose to transport source content, while a lightweight multiscale network predicts RGB residuals to correct artifacts and infer missing appearance. Cached source features further reduce repeated computation. On GS-render, residual refinement improves PSNR by 0.72~dB over pure warping; evaluations on captured and rendered scenes demonstrate low query latency. This separation of scene rendering from local view updates supports responsive scene exploration, with potential applications in augmented and virtual reality.

</details>


### [14] [SPLIT-RL: Staged Perception-Language Reasoning Training with Claim-Level Advantages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10889)
*Raja Kumar,Rajat Koner,Ritwick Chaudhry,Zhuowei Li,Nishant Sankaran,Yifan Xing*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SPLIT-RL，将视觉推理(VR)与语言推理(LR)分阶段训练，并引入 Claim-Level Advantage (CLA-GRPO) 进行细粒度优势分配；推理时仍为单次 CoT，平均准确率较 GRPO 提升 1.4–6.1 分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VL 推理同时需要从图像中提取准确信息（视觉推理 VR）和基于该信息推断答案（语言推理 LR）。现有可验证奖励强化学习（如 GRPO）用单一 CoT、仅以最终答案奖励训练，使所有 CoT token 共享同一序列级优势，无法区分不同能力导致的错误。

Method: SPLIT-RL 分阶段后训练：将 VR 与 LR 在互不重叠的阶段分别优化，由于同一组 rollout 仅在一个能力维度上有差异，组相对优势可隔离该能力，每阶段使用对应奖励。进一步提出 CLA-GRPO，在 VR 阶段将 rollout 分解为原子视觉断言（atomic visual claims），基于视觉类型的组构建给出断言级细粒度优势。训练分两阶段，但推理时与 GRPO 一样仅一次 CoT 调用。

Result: 在 Qwen3-VL 2B 至 30B-A3B 及 InternVL3.5-8B 上，SPLIT-RL 平均准确率较 GRPO 提升 1.4–6.1 分。基于 oracle 的能力诊断显示：answer-only GRPO 未改变感知能力，而 SPLIT-RL 同时提升 VR 与 LR。

Conclusion: 通过能力解耦的分阶段训练与断言级优势分配，可在不增加推理成本（仍为单次 CoT）的前提下，同时改善视觉推理与语言推理能力。

Abstract: Vision-Language (VL) reasoning requires a model to both extract relevant and accurate information from an image (visual reasoning, VR), and to infer the answer from it (language reasoning, LR). Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards typically trains both through a single chain-of-thought with a final-answer reward. This gives every CoT token the same sequence-level advantage, failing to distinguish capability specific errors. We propose SPLIT-RL, a staged post-training approach that trains VR and LR in disjoint phases. Because a group's rollouts differ along one capability at a time, the group-relative advantage isolates it, and each phase is optimized using phase-specific reward. We further introduce Claim-Level Advantage (CLA-GRPO), which decomposes VR-phase rollouts into atomic visual claims and provides a fine-grained advantage at claim level based on visual-type group formation. Although trained in two phases, trained policy is evaluated like GRPO model, with a single CoT call at inference time. Under this protocol, SPLIT-RL improves average accuracy over GRPO by 1.4-6.1 points across Qwen3-VL models from 2B to 30B-A3B and InternVL3.5-8B. Evaluating each capability using an oracle based diagnostic shows that answer-only GRPO leaves perception unchanged, whereas SPLIT-RL improves both VR and LR.

</details>


### [15] [Less from More: Reinforcing Sparse Video Reasoning from Dense References](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10893)
*Wenfang Sun,Yingjun Du,Cees G. M. Snoek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出SAVER，一个从稠密到稀疏的后训练框架，以稠密视频视图作为稀疏帧推理的训练期参考，通过强化后训练与针对性奖励，使稀疏视图预测保留任务相关时序证据，从而在有限时序证据下实现可靠推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频-语言模型常假设更多时序观测会带来更可靠的推理，但论文质疑该假设，认为关键挑战不是高效处理更多视频帧，而是在有限时序证据下学习可靠推理。

Method: SAVER采用稠密到稀疏的后训练框架：训练时同时优化成对的稠密与稀疏视图；在强化后训练中使用grounding奖励和可靠性门控的参考奖励，促使稀疏视图预测保留任务相关时序证据。训练仅使用1,250个随机采样的时序定位样本，不使用任何视频问答标注。

Result: 在三个时序定位基准和六个视频问答基准上，SAVER在不同帧预算下均持续提升性能；尤其能以显著更少的帧数匹配或超越稠密帧的Qwen3.5基线。

Conclusion: 结果表明，时序定位可以作为有效的证据定位代理，用于学习可迁移到更广泛视频理解任务的稀疏视频推理。

Abstract: Video-language models commonly assume that more temporal observations lead to more reliable reasoning. We question this assumption and argue that the key challenge is not merely processing more video frames efficiently, but learning to reason reliably under limited temporal evidence. We propose SAVER, a dense-to-sparse post-training framework that uses dense video views as training-time references for sparse-frame inference. During reinforcement post-training, paired dense and sparse views are optimized with grounding rewards and a reliability-gated reference reward, encouraging sparse view predictions to preserve task-relevant temporal evidence. Notably, SAVER is trained only on 1,250 randomly sampled temporal grounding examples, without using any video question answering annotations. Across three temporal grounding benchmarks and six video question-answering benchmarks, SAVER consistently improves performance across frame budgets. In particular, SAVER can match or surpass dense-frame Qwen3.5 baselines while using substantially fewer frames. These results show that temporal grounding can serve as an effective evidence-localization proxy for learning sparse video reasoning that transfers to broader video understanding tasks.

</details>


### [16] [GHARP: Real-time Gaussian Head Animation from Large-scale Reconstruction Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10945)
*Ali Benlalah,Sepehr Johari,Patricia Vitoria,Armin Kappeler,Artem Sevastopolsky,Alexander Jung,Gabriele Fanelli,Kevin Mader,Manuel Breitenstein,Claudia Plüss,Jan Rüegg,Simon Biland,Thomas Etterlin,Dmitry Kostiaev,Mathias Deschler,Brian Amberg,Sebastian Martin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GHARP 通过将头部驱动动画解耦为离线身份重建与实时在线动画两阶段，并在预训练重建模型的语义结构化隐空间中预测表情残差，同时引入身体对齐网络消除身体姿态与衣物位置带来的歧义，在 Ava-256 基准上达到最优质量，并在 A100 上最多快 13 倍、高斯点数减少 8 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的表情驱动数字人方法在实时性、保真度与运行开销之间难以兼顾；同时，由于表情编码只描述面部，未包含身体姿态与衣物位置信息，导致这些区域在输入中欠约束，动画网络面临病态映射，只能对冲突的身体外观取平均，从而产生模糊与时间闪烁。

Method: 方法分为三部分：(1) 身份阶段离线构建主体的几何与外观表示；(2) 动画阶段运行时在身份表示之上用轻量网络预测与表情相关的残差，该网络针对移动设备优化；(3) 动画在预训练重建模型的语义结构化隐空间中进行，使表情变化保持空间局部性，从而提升残差预测效率，并借助重建先验的一致空间布局将多视角输入融合为紧凑的固定尺寸规范高斯表示。针对身体欠约束问题，额外引入身体对齐网络，学习将目标图像中的人物身体与输入参考图像对齐，从而消除训练信号中的歧义。

Result: 在 Ava-256 基准上取得当前最优（state-of-the-art）的生成质量，同时在 A100 GPU 上运行速度最高提升 13 倍，且所用高斯点数量减少 8 倍。

Conclusion: 将身份重建与实时动画解耦、在语义结构化隐空间中预测残差，并辅以身体对齐网络，能够有效缓解表情驱动方法中身体区域欠约束导致的模糊与闪烁问题，在质量与效率上同时取得显著优势。

Abstract: We present GHARP (Real-time Gaussian Head Animation from Large-scale Reconstruction Prior), a method that animates 3D human heads in real time from a few input images of a subject and a driving expression signal. We decouple the problem into an identity stage that builds a representation of the subject's geometry and appearance offline, and an animation stage that predicts expression-dependent residuals on top of it at runtime. This separation offers a favorable trade-off with respect to fidelity, quality and runtime: the identity stage can be expensive while the animation stage runs a lightweight network, optimized for mobile devices. Our method performs animation in a semantically structured latent space of a pretrained reconstruction model, where expression changes remain spatially contained, making residual prediction efficient. This reconstruction prior provides a consistent spatial layout, allowing fusion of multiple input views into a compact, fixed-size canonical Gaussian representation. While this two-stage design improves the runtime-quality trade-off, it still inherits a problem common to all expression-driven avatar methods: expression codes describe only the face and thus omit body pose and clothing position, making these regions underspecified in the input. The animation network faces an ill-posed mapping and resorts to averaging over conflicting body appearances, producing blur and temporal flicker. We address this with a body alignment network that learns to align the person's body in the target image with the input reference images, removing the ambiguity from the training signal. Our method achieves state-of-the-art quality on the Ava-256 benchmark while running up to 13x faster on an A100 GPU with 8x fewer Gaussians.

</details>


### [17] [GPU-Accelerated Computation of Persistent Homology for Topological Analysis of Image Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10959)
*Fan Wang,Hubert Wagner,Rezaul Chowdhury,Chao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 TopoGPU，一种针对 2D/3D 图像立方复形的 GPU 流式持久同调计算流水线，平均端到端加速 53.24x（最高 198.01x）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久同调在深度学习中被广泛采用，但其计算是网络训练的主要瓶颈，现有方法（如 Cubical Ripser）速度不足。

Method: TopoGPU 以 chunk 为单位流式读入图像，用大规模并行 GPU 内核在 GPU block 网格上处理；边界关系累积到主机内存，由 CPU 执行边界矩阵约简。方法包含分层感知的离散 Morse 匹配（可证明在流式下保持持久同调）、并行拓扑排序算法与并行 V-path 奇偶算法以在 GPU 上导出 Morse 边界。

Result: 在所有评测基准上均优于 Cubical Ripser，平均端到端加速 53.24x，最高 198.01x；集成到保拓扑深度网络后显著降低训练中持久同调计算开销。

Conclusion: TopoGPU 有效缓解了持久同调的计算瓶颈，可用于保拓扑深度学习训练；已开源，提供预编译二进制、Colab notebook 与 Docker 镜像。

Abstract: In recent years, persistent homology has seen rapid adoption in deep learning, yet its computation remains a major bottleneck in network training. This paper introduces TopoGPU, a GPU streaming pipeline that computes persistence diagrams of cubical complexes induced by 2D and 3D images. TopoGPU streams the input image chunk by chunk, processing each chunk with massively parallel GPU kernels on a grid of GPU blocks; the resulting boundary relations are accumulated in host memory, where the CPU performs the boundary matrix reduction. TopoGPU introduces a stratification-aware discrete Morse matching that provably preserves persistent homology under streaming, together with a parallel topological sorting algorithm and a parallel V-path parity algorithm for deriving Morse boundaries on the GPU. TopoGPU outperforms Cubical Ripser, a state-of-the-art method for persistent homology computation, on every benchmark evaluated, achieving an average end-to-end speedup of 53.24x and a maximum of 198.01x. We further integrate TopoGPU into a topology-preserving deep network, demonstrating that it substantially reduces the cost of persistent homology computation during network training. TopoGPU is open source, with pre-built binaries, Google Colab notebooks, and Docker images available at the project's GitHub page: https://github.com/seravee08/GPU-Computation-of-Persistent-Homology-for-Image-Data.

</details>


### [18] [LVSPM: Long Sequence View Synthesis and Pose Estimation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10960)
*Xi Chen,Yachi Zhang,Linghao Chen,Minghua Liu,Hao Su,Zexiang Xu,Xiaoshuai Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LVSPM 是一个可泛化模型，联合估计相机位姿并从无标定图像集合合成新视角；仅用 RGB 图像与位姿监督训练，结合测试时训练层可扩展至数百视图，在多个数据集上超越 VGGT 的位姿估计与无位姿新视角合成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在新视角合成与位姿估计上依赖稠密 3D 真值或难以扩展至大量输入视图；同时在实际协议下，视图增多、场景扩大时基线方法性能崩溃，亟需一种可泛化、可扩展且无位姿依赖的高质量方案。

Method: 提出 LVSPM，联合估计相机位姿与合成新视角；仅使用 RGB 图像与位姿监督训练，避免稠密 3D 真值；引入测试时训练（TTT）层，实现从少量到数百输入视图的无缝扩展。

Result: 在 RealEstate10k、Co3Dv2、DL3DV 上，LVSPM 在 16–256 视图范围内位姿估计超过 VGGT，在严格阈值下优势尤为显著；在“更多视图覆盖更大场景”的实际协议下，其无位姿新视角合成达到 SOTA，PSNR 甚至超过依赖位姿的模型，并在场景规模增大时保持高质量，而基线方法出现崩溃。

Conclusion: LVSPM 验证了仅用 RGB 与位姿监督、借助 TTT 层即可实现可泛化、可扩展的联合位姿估计与新视角合成，在多项基准上取得领先表现，并具备随场景规模扩展的鲁棒性。

Abstract: We present LVSPM, a generalizable model that jointly estimates camera poses and synthesizes novel views from uncalibrated image collections. Trained with only RGB images and pose supervision, LVSPM avoids dense 3D ground truth and employs test-time training (TTT) layers to scale seamlessly to hundreds of input views. On RealEstate10k, Co3Dv2, and DL3DV, LVSPM surpasses VGGT in pose estimation across 16-256 views, with especially large margins at strict thresholds. For novel view synthesis under a practical protocol where more views cover larger scenes, LVSPM achieves state-of-the-art pose-free quality---surpassing even pose-dependent models in PSNR---and still maintains high quality as scene scale grows, while baselines collapse. The code is available at https://burningdust21.github.io/Projects/LVSPM .

</details>


### [19] [Fluid-Gen-Zero: Grounding Pretrained Video Generators in Physics without Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10984)
*Hong Huang,Yuqiu Liu,Chenyu You,Daniel Martin,Chuhang Zou,Wuyang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Fluid-Gen-Zero 是一个免训练框架，将物理推理与外观合成解耦，把运动动力学交给物理模拟器，同时保留预训练视频生成器的外观建模能力，通过两级智能体工作流（生成时规划与潜空间引导）实现物理感知的流体-物体交互视频生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型难以生成物理一致、符合真实动力学的流体-物体交互视频；直接在生成模型中编码物理规律代价高、泛化差，因此需要一种既能保证物理准确性又能保留高质量外观生成能力的方案。

Method: 提出免训练框架 Fluid-Gen-Zero，解耦物理推理与外观合成。将运动动力学委托给物理模拟器，保留预训练视频生成器的外观能力。通过两级智能体工作流连接两域：(1) 生成时规划——由 VLM 智能体解读意图与模拟 rollout 来组织生成片段；(2) 潜空间引导——通过区域感知潜空间包裹（region-aware latent wrapping）在去噪过程中注入模拟信号。该设计为即插即用，兼容现有视频基础模型。此外构建了流体-物体交互视频生成基准。

Result: 在 Tora（基于 CogVideoX）、VACE 与 WanMove（基于 Wan）上，Fluid-Gen-Zero 一致提升模拟对齐度：物体轨迹误差降低 26.7%–81.5%，流体 fEPE（流体流端点误差）降低 67.9%–84.0%，同时基本保持感知质量。人类偏好研究中，在三个骨干网络同骨干对比中 55.1%–74.4% 的样本偏好该框架，与基于模拟的方法相比偏好比例达 90.4%–94.2%。

Conclusion: Fluid-Gen-Zero 通过解耦物理推理与外观合成、结合智能体规划与潜空间引导，实现了即插即用、物理一致的流体-物体交互视频生成，在多个视频基础模型上显著提升模拟对齐并保持感知质量，代码与数据将在接收后发布。

Abstract: We present Fluid-Gen-Zero, a training-free framework for physics-aware fluid-object interaction video generation that decouples physical reasoning from appearance synthesis. Our key insight is to delegate motion dynamics to a physics simulator while preserving the appearance modeling capacity of pretrained video generators. We bridge these two domains through a two-level agentic workflow: generation-time planning, where a vision-language model (VLM) agent interprets intent and the simulation rollout to organize generation clips, and latent-space guidance, which injects simulation signals into denoising through region-aware latent wrapping. This plug-and-play design is compatible with current video foundation models. We further introduce a benchmark for fluid-object interaction video generation. Across Tora (CogVideoX-based), VACE and WanMove (Wan-based), Fluid-Gen-Zero consistently improves simulation alignment, reducing object trajectory error by 26.7%-81.5% and fluid fEPE (fluid flow endpoint error) by 67.9%-84.0%, while largely preserving perceptual quality. In a human preference study, raters favor Fluid-Gen-Zero in 55.1%-74.4% of same-backbone comparisons across three backbones, and in 90.4%-94.2% of comparisons against simulation-based methods. Code and data will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [20] [Omni-Diffusion-Distill: Few-Step Distillation of Unified Multimodal Diffusion Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10990)
*Hong Huang,Chenhongyi Yang,Junzhe Sun,Animesh Sinha,Wuyang Chen,Yifan Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Omni-Diffusion-Distill 两阶段统一蒸馏框架，将多模态离散扩散大语言模型的图像生成与多模态理解同时压缩至少步解码，实现 18.2x/21.2x 加速并保持高性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一多模态扩散大语言模型（dLLM）虽能单一架构兼顾图像生成与多模态理解，但迭代解码需数十至数百次前向传播。现有少步蒸馏方法多只针对图像生成或文本生成，如何将完全离散的多模态 dLLM 压缩为单一高效学生模型并同时保留生成与理解能力尚不明确。

Method: 两阶段统一蒸馏框架，在离散 token 空间中对图像与文本、生成与理解进行对齐蒸馏。第一阶段：学生通过回放缓存的教师轨迹学习跳步解码；第二阶段：在学生自身 rollout 的中间状态上精炼。此外引入两项改进：成对碰撞惩罚（pairwise collision penalty）以减少并行文本解码中的重复；熵匹配引导（entropy-matched guidance）以防止拟合尖锐化教师分布导致的图像生成熵崩溃。

Result: 在解码效率与性能权衡上达到 SOTA：图像生成从 128 步降至 8 步，多模态理解从 512 步降至 64 步，分别获得 18.2x 与 21.2x 墙钟加速。该预算下，文生图 GenEval 得 0.828、DPG-Bench 得 83.0；多模态理解 MM-Vet GPT 评判得 20.0、COCO captioning 得 57.2（同步骤下为教师 28.4 的两倍）。

Conclusion: Omni-Diffusion-Distill 证明可对完全离散的统一多模态 dLLM 进行单一学生模型的两阶段蒸馏，在离散 token 空间内统一生成与理解的蒸馏，并通过碰撞惩罚与熵匹配引导缓解退化，显著降低推理成本的同时保持甚至超越教师性能。

Abstract: Unified multimodal diffusion large language models (dLLMs) offer a single architecture for both image generation and multimodal understanding, but their iterative decoding requires tens to hundreds of forward passes. Existing few-step distillation methods largely focus on either image generation or text generation, making it unclear how to compress a fully discrete multimodal dLLM into a single efficient student while preserving both generation and understanding. We introduce Omni-Diffusion-Distill, a unified two-stage distillation framework that retains strong generation and understanding capabilities while substantially reducing the inference cost of a unified multimodal dLLM. Omni-Diffusion-Distill aligns the distillation of both generation and understanding, for both images and text, in the discrete token space. In the first stage, the student is trained to skip decoding steps by replaying cached teacher trajectories, and in the second stage the student is refined on intermediate states along its own rollouts. We further remedy two sources of degradation in unified distillation with a pairwise collision penalty that reduces repetition under parallel text decoding, and entropy-matched guidance that prevents entropy collapse caused by fitting the sharpened teacher distribution in image generation. Omni-Diffusion-Distill achieves state-of-the-art trade-offs between decoding efficiency and generation and understanding performance for multimodal dLLMs, reducing image generation from 128 to 8 decoding steps and multimodal understanding from 512 to 64, giving 18.2x and 21.2x wall-clock speedups. Under these budgets, it scores 0.828 on GenEval and 83.0 on DPG-Bench for text-to-image generation, while reaching GPT judge scores of 20.0 on MM-Vet and 57.2 on COCO captioning (twice the teacher's 28.4 at the same steps) for multimodal understanding.

</details>


### [21] [Region-Aware CLS Token Augmentation for Fine-Grained Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10991)
*Ian de Holanda Cavalcanti Bezerra,Vivek Trivedy,Lucas Pascotti Valem,Longin Jan Latecki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出用空间ROI token增强DINOv2-reg的语义token（[CLS]和register token），并基于ColBERT式多向量检索框架实现细粒度图像检索，在无外部框或显著性模块的情况下自动定位感兴趣区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅依赖单一全局语义描述符（如[CLS] token）会压缩图像所有语义信息，损害下游检索性能，尤其对细粒度检索任务影响明显。

Method: 在DINOv2-reg上，对每个"cue"token（[CLS]与各register token）匹配一个"buddy"图像patch token，并提取N×N patch区域生成局部化ROI token；无需外部边界框或显著性模块即可自动捕获关键区域。随后将这些token纳入受ColBERT启发的多向量检索框架，通过逐token对齐机制实现细粒度匹配，同时避免保留全部patch嵌入带来的巨大存储开销。

Result: 实验表明：(1) register token编码了可补充[CLS] token的有用细粒度细节；(2) 自动池化的ROI token进一步提升细粒度判别力；(3) 少量token的多向量检索优于DINOv2-reg单向量基线，且在大规模搜索中仍具可行性。

Conclusion: 通过将语义token与其空间区域表示相结合，并采用轻量多向量检索，可在不显著增加存储成本的前提下显著提升细粒度图像检索性能。

Abstract: Image retrieval methods often rely on a single global semantic descriptor extracted from an image, e.g., the [CLS] token in vision transformers. However, trying to squeeze all the semantic information of an image into a single descriptor can hurt downstream retrieval performance, especially for fine-grained retrieval tasks. In this work, we augment the semantic tokens in the newer visual transformers, the global [CLS] token and the four register tokens, with a carefully selected collection of spatial tokens, aiming to capture the spatial region representation that characterizes the contents captured in each of the semantic tokens. We leverage the DINOv2-reg model, which includes register tokens that emergently learn object and part-based representations. For each "cue" token ([CLS] and each register token), we find a "buddy" image patch token and extract an N x N patch region to produce a set of localized ROI tokens. Our approach automatically captures important regions of interest without any external bounding boxes or saliency modules, purely by matching semantic tokens with their spatial representation regions. Furthermore, we incorporate these tokens into a multi-vector retrieval framework inspired by ColBERT, enabling fine-grained matching via a per-token alignment mechanism while avoiding the large storage cost of keeping all patch embeddings. Through extensive experiments, we find that (1) register tokens encode useful fine-grained details that can complement the [CLS] token; (2) automatically pooled ROI tokens further improve fine-grained discrimination; and (3) multi-vector retrieval with a small set of tokens improves over a DINOv2-reg single-vector baseline while remaining tractable for large-scale search. The code is available at https://github.com/IdhcbIan/Augmenting_CLS_with_ROI_tokens.

</details>


### [22] [PCAsplat: Gaussian Splatting with Local PCA Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11011)
*Vitor Matias,Filipe Nascimento,Kiyohiro Nakayama,João Paulo Lima,Márcus Lobo,Gordon Wetzstein,Leonidas Guibas,Afonso Paiva,Tiago Novello*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PCAsplat：基于可微局部PCA的几何感知正则化框架，改善3DGS表面贴合、减少浮点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D高斯泼溅主要用光栅化损失优化，仅监督对采样相机光线有贡献的splat；被遮挡或贡献小的Gaussian几何梯度弱或无，易偏离真实表面，产生浮点（floaters）。

Method: 提出PCAsplat，基于可微局部主成分分析（PCA）的几何感知正则化：正则器直接作用于高斯中心邻域，能更新不参与当前训练视角的高斯；正则化PCA特征值，促使高斯移动到真实表面并具有各向同性切平面覆盖；将每个高斯法线对齐到PCA估计的邻域法线以强制方向一致。

Result: 在DTU、Tanks and Temples、NeRF Synthetic上实验表明，PCAsplat生成的splat更接近参考表面采样，显著减少浮点；表面贴合splat支持下游几何处理，包括点云分割和直接泊松重建；在新视角合成和网格提取等常规任务上仍具竞争力。

Conclusion: PCAsplat通过局部PCA几何正则化，缓解光栅化损失对无贡献高斯的弱监督问题，实现表面贴合的高斯表示，兼顾几何质量与常规重建/合成性能，代码将开源。

Abstract: Gaussian splatting has emerged as a flexible representation for 3D reconstruction from posed images. However, existing methods are optimized primarily using rasterization-based losses, which supervise a splat only when it contributes to sampled camera rays. Gaussians that are occluded or contribute little to the sampled view therefore receive weak or no geometric gradients and may drift away from the underlying surface, producing undesired floaters. We introduce PCAsplat, a geometry-aware regularization framework for Gaussian splatting based on differentiable local principal component analysis (PCA). Our PCA regularizer acts directly on neighborhoods of Gaussian centers and can therefore update Gaussians that do not contribute to the current training view. We regularize the PCA eigenvalues to encourage Gaussians to move to the underlying surface with isotropic tangent-plane coverage. We also align each Gaussian normal with the PCA-estimated neighborhood normal to enforce consistent orientation. Experiments on DTU, Tanks and Temples, and NeRF Synthetic show that the splats produced by PCAsplat better approximate samples of the reference surface while substantially reducing undesired floaters. These surface-aligned splats enable downstream geometry-processing tasks, including point cloud segmentation, and direct Poisson reconstruction. Additionally, PCAsplat remains competitive under conventional novel view synthesis and mesh extraction tasks. Code will be released.

</details>


### [23] [Mid-Training Language Models on Raw Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11019)
*Jaedong Hwang,Xiaoqian Shen,Ernie Chang,Changsheng Zhao,Chong Zhou,Saksham Suri,Qi Qian,Zechun Liu,Lemeng Wu,Qinsi Wang,Raghuraman Krishnamoorthi,Wei Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 纯原始视频（无字幕、无文本损失）可作为预训练语言模型的中间训练数据；以"预测下一视觉token"为目标对Qwen3-1.7B进行中间训练后，在4个视频与10个图像基准上分别平均提升2.9和5.1分，且14个文本基准表现不降反略升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型主要依赖配对的图文数据或带标注视频进行学习，而原始网络视频很少被用于继续训练已有的语言模型。作者希望验证：在无字幕、无文本损失的条件下，原始视频能否作为预训练语言模型的中间训练（mid-training）数据，从而以纯自监督方式注入视觉能力而不损害语言能力。

Method: 将视频帧编码为连续视觉token，让语言模型学习预测下一个视觉token。以Qwen3-1.7B为基础模型，使用YT-Temporal-1B中的原始视频片段进行中间训练；随后对中间训练模型与未做中间训练的模型施加相同的图文指令微调，使两者仅在中间训练环节存在差异。此外还对比了"预测字幕"与"预测下一视觉token"两种目标。

Result: 中间训练模型在4个视频基准上平均高2.9分，在10个图像基准（涵盖感知、文档、图表任务）上平均高5.1分。文本能力得以保持：14个文本基准平均48.9，未中间训练模型为48.0。训练过程分析显示，图像与视频增益在训练进度30%内即出现，此后趋于平台，波动小于0.5分。预测字幕的效果并不优于下一视觉token预测。

Conclusion: 原始视频可有效作为语言模型的中间训练数据，且可保持纯自监督，无需自动字幕生成带来的额外计算开销与标注噪声；视觉能力增益出现早且稳定，语言能力未被牺牲。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models learn mostly from paired image-text data or annotated video, and raw web video is rarely used to further train an existing language model. We study whether raw video, with no captions and no text loss, can serve as mid-training data for a pretrained language model. Frames are encoded into continuous visual tokens, and the language model learns to predict the next visual token. We mid-train Qwen3-1.7B on raw clips from YT-Temporal-1B and then apply the same image-text instruction tuning to it and to the model without mid-training, so that the two differ only in mid-training. The mid-trained model scores 2.9 points higher on average across four video benchmarks and 5.1 points higher across ten image benchmarks, spanning perception, document, and chart tasks. Text performance is preserved even though mid-training includes no text, with an average of 48.9 across 14 text benchmarks compared with 48.0 for the model without mid-training. Analyses across training show that the image and video gains emerge within 30% of training and plateau thereafter, varying by less than 0.5 points. Predicting captions fails to outperform next-visual-token prediction, demonstrating that video mid-training can remain purely self-supervised without the computational overhead or labeling noise of automated captioning.

</details>


### [24] [Expression-Diverse References for Identity-Preserving Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11023)
*Tianwen Fu,Wenbin Teng,Gonglin Chen,Junyi Ouyang,Haolin Xiong,Yajie Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究身份保持视频生成中单张参考人像的局限，提出表达多样性参考库用于生成与评估，在三种表情强度下均提升身份相似度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单张参考人像仅记录一个面部构型下的外观，当表情变化时，外观会以高度身份特异的方式改变，使未见表情下的外观欠定；同时这种表情相关变化也让评估复杂化——即使是同一真实人物的图像，在强表情下与中性参考的相似度也会下降。

Method: 从生成与评估两方面入手：利用受控照片与MEAD视频量化人脸识别相似度随表情强度的变化；构建紧凑且富表达力的参考库以捕捉多样的表情相关面部构型；扩展Stand-In模型以条件化于该表情多样参考集，并设计数据筛选流程从训练视频中提取一致且多样的人脸裁剪；在仅有单张人像时，用预训练面部重演模型合成额外表情以构建参考集。

Result: 在自建受控基准上，真实与合成参考集在轻度、强烈、极端三种表情强度下的身份相似度均优于所评估的基线，且在极端表情下提升最大。

Conclusion: 单参考下的身份保持存在表情相关欠定性与评估偏差，采用表情多样参考库并在分强度报告指标，可同时改善生成质量与评估可靠性，极端表情下收益最显著。

Abstract: Identity-preserving video generation aims to maintain a subject's identity while synthesizing realistic videos. Yet a single reference portrait captures the subject's appearance under only one facial configuration. As expressions change, facial appearance can vary in highly identity-specific ways, leaving the subject's appearance under unseen expressions underdetermined by the reference alone. This expression-dependent variation also complicates evaluation: similarity to a neutral reference may decrease under strong expressions even for real images of the same person. We investigate this limitation from both generation and evaluation perspectives. First, we quantify how face-recognition similarity varies with expression intensity using controlled photographs and MEAD videos. We then construct a compact yet expressive reference gallery that captures diverse expression-dependent facial configurations. Matching against this gallery provides a more robust measure of identity similarity under expressive motion. To further expose performance degradation with expression intensity, we report identity similarity separately for mild, intense, and extreme expressions. For generation, we extend Stand-In to condition on our expression-diverse reference sets and develop a data-curation pipeline that extracts consistent yet diverse face crops from training videos. In practical settings where only a single portrait is available, we construct the reference set by synthesizing additional expressions with a pretrained facial reenactment model. On our controlled benchmark, both real and synthesized reference sets outperform the evaluated baselines in identity similarity across all three expression-intensity regimes, with the largest improvements for extreme expressions.

</details>


### [25] [Transforming Image Editors into Video Editors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11037)
*Feng Wang,Zijie Li,Ceyuan Yang,Alan Yuille,Peng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Anchor-based Video Editing（AVE）两阶段框架，将强图像编辑器与运动引导图生视频扩散模型结合，避免端到端视频编辑训练，在IVEBench和VIE-Bench上取得良好性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频编辑相比图像编辑难度大、成本高。作者希望避免训练单体式端到端视频编辑器，转而复用已有强图像编辑器的语义理解、视觉保真与指令跟随能力。

Method: 基于"视频编辑可分解为稀疏关键帧编辑+跨时间传播"的洞察，提出两阶段AVE框架：第一阶段由强图像编辑器对选定关键帧进行组合式编辑；第二阶段由运动引导的图像到视频扩散模型将编辑后的关键帧视为固定锚点生成最终视频。

Result: 在IVEBench和VIE-Bench上，AVE在指令跟随、时序一致性和内容保真方面表现强劲。消融实验显示最终视频编辑质量与图像编辑器质量高度相关。

Conclusion: 将强图像编辑器通过锚定生成迁移到视频，是低成本且有效的视频编辑路径；未来视频编辑的进展可能来自更强的图像编辑基础及轻量级向视频的迁移。

Abstract: Recent image editing systems have achieved impressive semantic understanding, visual fidelity, and instruction-following ability, while video editing remains substantially more difficult and costly. In this paper, we present a simple alternative to end-to-end video editing: instead of training a monolithic video editor, we transform a strong image editor into a video editor through anchor-based generation. Our key insight is that video editing can be decomposed into two subproblems: editing a sparse set of keyframes and propagating those edits across time. Based on this observation, we propose Anchor-based Video Editing (AVE), a two-stage framework in which a powerful image editor first performs composed editing on selected keyframes, and a motion-guided image-to-video diffusion model then generates the final video by treating the edited keyframes as fixed anchors. This design directly inherits the strengths of modern image editors while avoiding expensive end-to-end video editing training. Experiments on IVEBench and VIE-Bench show that AVE achieves strong performance in instruction following, temporal consistency, and content fidelity. Further ablations reveal that final video editing quality is strongly correlated with the quality of the image editor, suggesting that future progress in video editing may come from stronger image editing foundations and lightweight transfer to video. Code is available at https://github.com/wangf3014/AVE.

</details>


### [26] [Rendering-Free Lookahead for Question-Guided Active Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11039)
*Koya Sakamoto,Daichi Azuma,Shuhei Kurita,Naoya Chiba,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo,Taiki Miyanishi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Rendering-Free Lookahead (RFL)：一种基于"可回答性"预测的相机运动选择策略，训练时用 3DGS 渲染未来视角并由冻结 VLM 打标，部署时无需渲染即可排序候选运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视角相关问题（如"盒子里有什么"）需要主动控制相机揭示当前视角下被遮挡的关键视觉证据。VLM 虽能解读已观测图像，但选择何种相机运动需要预判"未见视角"的价值，这一前瞻能力在部署时因无法渲染未来视角而难以直接获得。

Method: 将视角有用性量化为"可回答性"（answerability，即 VLM 判断某视角是否足以回答问题的置信估计）。RFL 通过特权式视觉前瞻把未来视角的价值从部署迁移到离线训练：训练阶段在 3D Gaussian Splatting 场景中渲染候选未来视角，用冻结 VLM 计算一步与两步 answerability 目标；再经两阶段蒸馏，训练学生模型从问题、近期视觉观测和候选相机运动预测这些动作价值。部署时直接以预测值排序选择相机运动，无需渲染未来视图。

Result: 在未见环境的 377 个 E3VS-Bench 测试回合上，RFL 相比使用同一 VLM 的直接动作基线，平均评判得分提升 43%。

Conclusion: 结果支持从特权式视觉前瞻中学习相机控制策略，用于视角相关的视觉问答；该方法无需在部署时进行未来视角渲染即可实现有效的前瞻性视角选择。

Abstract: Active robot vision requires controlling the camera to reveal task-relevant information that is hidden from the current viewpoint. For example, determining what is inside a box may require raising the camera and looking down into it. For viewpoint-dependent question answering, the challenge is to select camera motions that expose the visual evidence needed to answer the question. Although vision-language models (VLMs) can interpret observed images, selecting such motions requires anticipating the usefulness of unseen views. We quantify this usefulness as answerability, a VLM's estimate that a view suffices to answer the question, and present Rendering-Free Lookahead (RFL), a viewpoint-selection policy that ranks candidate camera motions by predicted future answerability. RFL transfers visual lookahead from deployment to offline training. At training, a privileged teacher renders candidate future views in 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) scenes and uses a frozen VLM to compute one- and two-step answerability targets. Through two-stage distillation, a student learns to predict these action values from the question, recent visual observations, and a candidate camera motion. At deployment, RFL uses these predicted values to select camera motions without rendering future views. On 377 E3VS-Bench test episodes in unseen environments, RFL improves the mean judge score by 43\% over a direct-action baseline using the same VLM. These results support learning camera-control policies from privileged visual lookahead for viewpoint-dependent question answering.

</details>


### [27] [SatFix: Absolute Visual Localization of UAVs in Satellite Maps from a Single Oblique Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11049)
*Jiarui Zeng,Kun Shi,Chiman Vong,Zhedong Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SatFix前馈式无人机绝对度量定位框架，从图像直接回归卫星地图坐标系下的连续2D位置与朝向，并构建University-Metric基准；单视图50m内定位成功率52.08%，中位位置误差45.66m，九视图降至21.96m。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨视角地理定位方法依赖候选库检索最相似卫星瓦片并报告Recall@K，定位结果受候选库采样方式影响，不提供朝向估计，且仅返回瓦片索引而非连续坐标，难以满足无人机绝对度量定位需求。

Method: 基于VGGT-Ω构建SatFix：以卫星网格特征作为查询聚合无人机视觉证据，两个轻量头在卫星地图坐标系下回归3-DoF位姿（连续2D位置与朝向）；无需显式3D地图、渲染鸟瞰图、辅助传感器或测试时位姿对齐；单一模型同时支持单视图与多视图输入，多视图训练中引入轨迹约束。同时构建University-Metric基准，卫星影像边长扩展至原University-1652瓦片的10.7倍（面积约114倍），并为原始无人机航迹提供连续位置与朝向标签。

Result: 单视图下，SatFix在50m内定位52.08%测试帧、10m内17.34%，中位位置误差45.66m、中位朝向误差20.81°；NVIDIA RTX 4090上单次单视图推理耗时低于0.1s。九视图下中位位置误差降至21.96m，中位朝向误差降至8.73°。相比微调VGGT-Ω基线，中位位置误差降低34.0%，九视图朝向误差从25.43°降至8.73°。

Conclusion: SatFix以端到端前馈方式实现跨视角无人机–卫星绝对度量定位，输出连续坐标与朝向，兼具单/多视图泛化性与实时推理效率，并通过University-Metric提供了具备连续位姿标签的大范围评测方案。

Abstract: We study absolute metric UAV localization within a provided geo-referenced satellite region, recovering continuous map position and viewing heading from a single oblique image or a short multi-view clip. Existing cross-view geo-localization methods retrieve the most similar satellite tile from a gallery and report Recall@K, but retrieval depends on gallery sampling, provides no heading estimate, and returns a tile index rather than a continuous coordinate. We propose SatFix, a feed-forward UAV--satellite localization framework built on VGGT-$Ω$. Satellite-grid features act as queries that aggregate UAV visual evidence, and two lightweight heads regress a 3-DoF pose in the satellite-map frame: continuous 2D position and heading. SatFix requires no explicit 3D map, rendered bird's-eye image, auxiliary sensor, or test-time pose alignment. A single model supports both single- and multi-view inputs, with trajectory constraints used during multi-view training. For metric evaluation, we introduce University-Metric, where satellite imagery is re-collected over a region up to 10.7$\times$ longer on a side (about 114$\times$ the ground area) than the original University-1652 tiles, with continuous position and heading labels for the original UAV tours. With one UAV view, SatFix localizes 52.08% of test frames within 50 m and 17.34% within 10 m, with median position and heading errors of 45.66 m and $20.81^\circ$, respectively. Inference takes under 0.1 s per single-view query on an NVIDIA RTX 4090. With nine UAV views, the median position error falls to 21.96 m and the median heading error to $8.73^\circ$. Compared with a fine-tuned VGGT-$Ω$ baseline, SatFix reduces median position error by 34.0% and nine-view median heading error from $25.43^\circ$ to $8.73^\circ$.

</details>


### [28] [Learning What to Trust in Multimodal Learning under Noisy Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11057)
*Jiashuo Zou,Xiaobo Xia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出REFINE，一种多模态标签噪声检测框架，联合利用融合表示与单模态表示进行噪声检测，通过判别特征向量构建与可信表示空间选择提升分类器泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态分类依赖高质量真实标签，而实际场景难以获得；传统噪声标签学习方法多聚焦单模态，未能充分利用多模态信息，因此需要构建更可靠的多模态噪声检测器。

Method: 理论分析表示结构与噪声检测能力的关系；在此基础上提出REFINE：对目标类与背景类进行判别分析以构建判别特征向量，为每个类别选择噪声检测能力更优的可信表示空间；在每个可信空间内度量实例表示与判别特征向量的对齐程度，并融合各空间筛出的子集，为多模态分类器提供更干净的监督信号。

Result: 在多种任务上的大量实验表明，REFINE相较基线方法具有更优性能。

Conclusion: 通过可信表示空间选择与融合表示、单模态表示协同检测标签噪声，可减少误标注样本的影响，提升模型泛化能力；源代码将公开。

Abstract: Multimodal classification processes and relates information from multiple modalities to achieve more accurate predictions. However, existing methods typically rely on high-quality ground-truth labels, which are difficult to obtain in real-world scenarios. While sample-selection methods for learning with noisy labels aim to identify correctly labeled examples from noisy data, traditional methods primarily focus on unimodal settings and fail to exploit multimodal information fully. This motivates us to build a more reliable noise detector in multimodal learning. To this end, we theoretically analyze the relationship between representation structure and noise detection capability. Based on this analysis, we propose REFINE, which is a multimodal label-noise detection framework that jointly uses fused and unimodal representations for label-noise detection. Specifically, REFINE constructs discriminative eigenvectors through discriminative analysis of the target and background classes and selects trusted representation spaces with better noise detection capability for each class. Within each trusted space, REFINE measures the alignment between each instance representation and the discriminative eigenvectors. It then combines the subsets selected from these spaces. The combined set provides cleaner supervision for updating the multimodal classifier, thereby reducing the influence of mislabeled examples during training and improving model generalization. Extensive experiments across diverse tasks demonstrate REFINE's superiority compared to baseline methods. The source code will be publicly available.

</details>


### [29] [Refine Connections, Close the Gap: A Reliable Enhancement Framework for Driving Scene Topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11058)
*Xiaoqi Wang,Dingyi Zhaung,David Paz,Wenbin He,Yucai Bai,Peng Zhou,Rui Zhang,Jinhua Zhao,Liu Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 TopoEnhance，一个即插即用的拓扑增强框架，将拓扑增强建模为去噪式重构过程，从随机扰动的真值图中恢复结构一致性，从而生成可靠的离散拓扑图。实验表明该方法在多种基线上同时提升连续指标（TOP score）与离散连通性指标（TJS），且无需重训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶需理解场景拓扑（车道与交通元素间的连通性）以支持安全路径规划与运动控制。现有方法虽能检测单个地图元素，但其连通性推理远未达到理论上限，检测基础上本可实现的性能存在明显差距。此外，传递给下游任务的决策型拓扑图常不可靠：主流做法通过对连续拓扑分数阈值化来导出连通性，但该分数难以反映真实的逻辑连通概率，导致误连或漏连；现有基准也主要以连续指标评估，忽视了决策所需的离散连通性。

Method: 作者提出 TopoEnhance 拓扑增强框架，旨在释放现有方法潜力并提升决策型拓扑可靠性。核心思路是将拓扑增强形式化为基于去噪的重构过程：模型学习从随机损坏的真值图中恢复结构一致性，从而解决逻辑不一致、纠正不可靠连接，输出稳健的离散拓扑图。该方法与来源无关（source-agnostic），可作为灵活模块作用于多种现有基线而无需重训练。为评估离散连通性，作者还提出了适配的 Topology Jaccard Similarity（TJS）指标。

Result: 在多种不同基线上的大量实验表明，TopoEnhance 持续提升连续拓扑指标（TOP score）以及由适配的 TJS 指标衡量的离散连通性。作为灵活、与来源无关的框架，TopoEnhance 在多种最先进基线上均带来显著增益，且无需重训练。

Conclusion: TopoEnhance 通过去噪式重构有效弥合了现有连通性推理与理论性能上限之间的差距，解决了连续分数阈值化带来的不可靠问题，并推动评估从纯连续指标转向决策所需的离散连通性。其即插即用、与来源无关的特性使其具备良好的通用性与实用价值。

Abstract: In autonomous driving, understanding scene topology - the connectivity between lanes and traffic elements - is critical for safe path planning and motion control. While current methods excel at detecting individual map elements, their connectivity reasoning often falls short of its theoretical potential, leaving a significant performance gap relative to the theoretical upper-bound achievable given the underlying detections. Furthermore, the decision-ready topology graphs passed to downstream tasks often remain unreliable. Current approaches typically derive connectivity by thresholding continuous topology scores; however, these scores often fail to reflect the true logical likelihood of connectivity, resulting in false positives or missing connections. Existing benchmarks further overlook this issue by primarily evaluating continuous metrics, rather than assessing the discrete connectivity required for decision-making. To bridge these gaps, we propose TopoEnhance, a novel topology enhancement framework designed to unlock the latent potential of existing methods and improve the reliability of decision-ready topology. We formulate topology enhancement as a denoising-based reconstruction process, where the model learns to recover structural consistency from stochastically corrupted ground-truth graphs. This formulation enables the model to resolve logical inconsistencies and rectify unreliable connections, producing robust discrete topology graphs that closely approach theoretical maximum performance. Extensive experiments across different baselines show that TopoEnhance consistently improves both continuous topology metrics (TOP score), and discrete connectivity measured by our adapted Topology Jaccard Similarity (TJS) metric. As a flexible, source-agnostic framework, TopoEnhance delivers substantial gains across diverse state-of-the-art baselines without requiring retraining.

</details>


### [30] [AffordDrive3D: Affordance-Aware World-Action Modeling with Spatial Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11060)
*Tianhui Cai,Xinglong Sun,Chao Fang,Zhenxin Li,Rui Song,Jose M. Alvarez,Yunxiang Mao,Jiaqi Ma,Langechuan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 AffordDrive3D，一种可行驶区域（affordance）与几何感知的世界-动作模型，联合预测未来动作相关区域与空间结构，在 NAVSIM 上取得 91.3 PDMS 与 89.9 EPDMS 的 SOTA 表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型多依赖 RGB 外观预测未来，近期引入几何预测以增强空间理解，但稠密几何仅描述整体空间布局，无法指示哪些区域与自车动作最相关。驾驶场景中模型还需识别并预测可安全行驶的区域及潜在碰撞风险区域，因此需要同时建模"动作相关区域"与其空间结构。

Method: 提出 AffordDrive3D：基于 VLM 主干以捕捉场景语义与驾驶上下文，预测直接影响自车运动的可行驶区域与碰撞关键区域；同时从 RGB 世界模型隐变量中预测未来几何。通过联合学习未来 affordance 与几何来支撑轨迹规划。

Result: 在 NAVSIM 基准上达到 SOTA，取得 91.3 PDMS 和 89.9 EPDMS。

Conclusion: 联合建模未来可行驶区域与几何结构能有效提升轨迹规划性能，验证了 affordance 与几何感知世界-动作模型的优越性。

Abstract: World-action models have recently improved autonomous driving by jointly learning future scene prediction and trajectory generation. Most existing approaches model the future primarily through RGB appearance, and recent works have begun to incorporate geometric prediction to improve spatial understanding. However, dense geometry describes the spatial layout of the entire scene without indicating which parts are most relevant to the ego vehicle's action. For driving, the model must also identify and anticipate where it can safely move and which regions may pose collision risks. Jointly modeling action-relevant regions and future geometry can provide the policy with both driving-relevant cues and their corresponding spatial structure. We therefore propose AffordDrive3D, an affordance- and geometry-aware world-action model that jointly learns future action-relevant regions and spatial structure. In order to capture the scene semantics and driving context needed for driving affordance prediction, we build AffordDrive3D on a VLM backbone to forecast drivable areas and collision-critical regions that directly affect ego motion, while predicting future geometry from RGB world-model latents. On NAVSIM, AffordDrive3D achieves state-of-the-art performance with 91.3 PDMS and 89.9 EPDMS, demonstrating the effectiveness of jointly modeling future affordances and geometry for trajectory planning.

</details>


### [31] [Diffusion Meta-Prompting and Steering for Generalizable Foundation Model Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11067)
*Deepak Sridhar,Yi Li,Kartikeya Bhardwaj,Shuangjun Liu,Taotao Jing,Yuan Li,Shuai Zhang,Jiancheng Lyu,Dashan Gao,Nuno Vasconcelos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出扩散元提示（DMP）框架，用扩散模型对已学提示的分布建模，在无需原始任务样本或损失的情况下合成新提示，并通过测试时引导策略提升采样稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示学习（prompt learning）得到的提示通常高度任务特定，难以泛化到新类别、新领域或任务组合，且提示检索方法存储与推理成本高。

Method: 将已学提示的集合视为数据，用扩散模型学习其分布；训练与采样均不访问原始任务样本或任务损失，仅以自然语言任务描述为条件生成新提示。引入测试时引导（test-time steering）策略，在扩散采样中以仓库中训练阶段选出的最优提示作为潜在锚点，无需重训练或访问测试类别。

Result: 在分类、检索和文本到图像生成任务上提升泛化能力；支持概念组合与负向提示且无需显式训练；相比提示检索方法存储与推理成本降低超90%；复合分类在55对数据集上平均提升最高2.0%，个别组合（如Eurosat与Flowers）提升达8.5%；层级分类跨任务泛化提升约2-9%；并给出DMP采样提示期望任务损失的理論界。代码已开源。

Conclusion: DMP通过建模提示分布实现可泛化的提示合成，兼顾概念组合、负向提示与低成本，并提供理论保证，是一种有效且高效的提示生成框架。

Abstract: Prompt learning is a popular method for adapting foundation models, but learned prompts are typically task-specific and fail to generalize to new classes, domains, or compositions of tasks. In this paper, we introduce a Diffusion Meta-Prompt (DMP) model , a framework that models the distribution of learned prompts using diffusion models. Given a repository of previously learned prompts, DMP is trained and sampled without access to the original task examples or task losses, and synthesizes new prompts conditioned on natural language task descriptions. To improve the sampling stability, we introduce a test-time steering strategy for DMP, which uses the best training-selected prompt in the repository as a latent anchor during diffusion sampling, without retraining the DMP or accessing test classes. DMP improves generalization across classification, retrieval and text-to-image generation tasks, supports concept composition and negative prompting without explicit training. It reduces storage and inference costs by over 90% compared to prompt retrieval methods. For composite classification, DMP achieves upto 2.0% average gain over prior meta-learning methods across 55 pairs of datasets with gains as high as 8.5% on specific pairs such as Eurosat and Flowers. DMP also enhances cross-task generalization with ~2-9% improvement for hierarchical classification task. We further provide a theoretical guarantee bounding the expected task loss of prompts sampled from a DMP. Code is available: https://github.com/DeepakSridhar/dmp

</details>


### [32] [No Distillation Needed: Single-Pass Real-Time Talking Heads via Acausal Noise Shaping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11070)
*Yu Han,Dejan Markovic,Alexander Richard,Wojciech Zielonka,Akshay Venkatesh,Cheng-hsin Wuu,Michael Zollhoefer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FaceGAN 提出用单步 GAN 替代扩散模型实现音频驱动的流式面部动画，通过非因果噪声路径设计解决随机结构限制，单次前向即输出表情与头姿，质量达 SOTA 且可无限生成而不漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频驱动的面部动画需在线流式运行，而当前主流扩散模型每样本需多次网络评估，不适合流式场景。作者认为该领域中音频条件下的面部运动处于相对低维流形，单步 GAN 已足够，真正障碍不是容量而是随机结构。

Method: 首先从理论上证明：由独立同分布噪声驱动的因果、时不变生成器，无法在压制某频段输出谱的同时避免每步创新（per-step innovation）崩塌。为此提出 FaceGAN，通过非因果地塑造噪声通路来化解该限制——由于驱动噪声是合成的，其未来可提前采样，因此音频到表情的路径仍保持因果，模型支持完全因果运行。

Result: FaceGAN 每帧单次前向传播即输出表情与头部姿态，生成质量匹配或超越当前最先进方法；因其为前馈结构并采用有界注意力窗口，可无限生成而不产生漂移。

Conclusion: 在流式音频驱动面部动画中，扩散模型的多次评估并非必要；通过非因果噪声塑造，单步 GAN 可在保持因果性的同时实现高质量、无漂移的实时生成，为在线虚拟形象与具身智能体提供高效方案。

Abstract: Audio-driven facial animation underpins real-time avatars, telepresence, and embodied virtual agents. And it must run online: each frame emitted from audio observed up to the current time, at interactive rates. Recent progress is dominated by diffusion models, which need many network evaluations per sample and are therefore a poor fit for streaming. We argue the cost is unnecessary in this domain. Audio-conditioned facial motion occupies a comparatively low-dimensional manifold, a regime where a single-pass GAN suffices. The obstacle is not capacity but stochastic structure. We show that a causal, time-invariant generator driven by i.i.d. noise cannot suppress its output spectrum over a band without collapsing its per-step innovation. We proposed FaceGAN, which dissolved the limitation by shaping the noise pathway acausally. Because the driving noise is synthetic, its future can be sampled now, so the audio-to-expression path stays causal, and the model supports fully causal operation. FaceGAN emits expression and head pose in a single forward pass per frame and matches or outperforms state-of-art approaches in generation quality. Being feed-forward with bounded attention windows, it generates indefinitely without drift.

</details>


### [33] [Contrast Enhancement or Noise Reduction? On Improving Cervical Cancer Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11086)
*Ach Khozaimi,Ulfatun Nahdhiyah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究系统评估了图像预处理对CNN宫颈癌巴氏涂片分类性能的影响，发现CLAHE对比度增强比PMD滤波去噪更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宫颈癌是全球主要致死原因之一，深度学习在医学图像分类中表现良好，但图像预处理算法对分类性能的影响在文献中研究不足，因此需要评估预处理对CNN巴氏涂片分类的作用。

Method: 使用SIPaKMeD数据集，训练并评估三种CNN架构（ResNet-34、MobileNet-V2、DenseNet-121），应用两种预处理算法：PMD滤波去噪和CLAHE对比度增强，并通过混淆矩阵评估性能。

Result: 预处理提升了所有模型的分类性能。CLAHE显著提高ResNet-34和DenseNet-121的准确率，均从76.73%升至84.16%；PMD滤波改进有限，且略微降低了MobileNet-V2的性能。

Conclusion: 该研究系统比较了多种CNN架构下的对比度增强与去噪技术，表明对比度增强在提升CNN性能方面优于去噪，并为改进宫颈癌分类提供了新的处理流程。

Abstract: Purpose: Cervical cancer is one of the leading causes of mortality worldwide. Deep learning has shown promising performance in medical image classification. The influence of image preprocessing algorithms on classification performance remains insufficiently investigated in the literature. This research aims to evaluate the impact of image preprocessing algorithms on the performance of CNNs for Pap smear image classification.
  Methods: Three CNN architectures (ResNet-34, MobileNet-V2, and DenseNet-121) were trained and evaluated using the SIPaKMeD dataset. Two preprocessing algorithms were applied: the PMD filter for noise reduction and CLAHE for contrast enhancement. The model performance was assessed using a confusion matrix.
  Results: Preprocessing improved the classification performance of all models. CLAHE significantly increased the accuracy of ResNet-34 from 76.73% to 84.16% and DenseNet-121 from 76.73% to 84.16%. The PMD filter yielded limited improvement and slightly reduced the MobileNet-V2 performance.
  Novelty: This research provides a systematic comparison of contrast enhancement and noise reduction techniques across CNN architectures. This research demonstrates that contrast enhancement is more effective than noise reduction in improving CNN performance. The research provides new pipelines for improving cervical cancer classification.

</details>


### [34] [Continuous Ground-Truth Construction and a Recovery Policy for Air--Water Robotic Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11096)
*Jiangong Xiao,Zhe Sun,Kanzhong Yao,Yuanbo Bi,Haofei Zhao,Ruixuan Hu,Guan Huang,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出跨介质（空气-水界面）水下目标跟踪评测集与跨介质恢复策略CMRP，在不重训练视觉主干的前提下提升MixFormerV2精度与恢复速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
