Motivation: 空气-水界面跟踪受水花、气泡、折射、反射及目标外观突变影响，观测会暂时失效（视觉盲区），由此产生两个耦合难题：一是仅用图像标注难以可靠描述盲区期间目标的真实物理位置，评测本身缺乏可信真值；二是在线跟踪中受污染观测会污染运动估计与外观模板，导致跟踪漂移或丢失。

Method: （1）构建评测集：同步相机帧与动作捕捉位姿，投影已知目标几何，用介质门控残差（medium-gated residual）校正水下投影，并对标注进行人工复核，得到仅用于评测的跨介质测试集共22,346帧。（2）提出跨介质恢复策略CMRP：以置信度触发模板选择，向MixFormerV2同时提供固定初始模板、窗口内最优的触发前模板、以及触发帧的Kalman引导图像裁剪及其对应权重，且不重新训练视觉主干。

Result: 精度评测中CMRP取得49.90 Macro Success AUC，比MixFormerV2 Official高2.95个点；在选定的跨介质过渡与遮挡恢复区间上，CMRP将MixFormerV2相对于Official更新的跟踪覆盖率从47.91%提升至50.43%，同时成功恢复视频的平均丢失-恢复时延从55.3帧降至49.3帧。

Conclusion: 通过位姿同步与介质门控投影校正构建可信的跨介质评测基准，并以置信度驱动的模板选择策略在不重训练主干的情况下实现更优精度、更高跟踪覆盖与更低恢复时延，为跨介质视觉跟踪的评测与在线鲁棒性提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Visual tracking across the air-water interface is challenged by splashes, bubbles, refraction, reflections, and abrupt appearance changes that can temporarily invalidate observations. This setting poses two coupled difficulties: first, for evaluation, image-only annotation cannot reliably describe the target's physical location during visual blindness; second, for online tracking, corrupted observations can contaminate motion estimates and appearance templates. We address the first difficulty with a construction pipeline that synchronizes camera frames with motion-capture poses, projects known target geometry, corrects underwater projection with a medium-gated residual, and subjects the annotations to manual review. This yields an evaluation-only cross-medium test set of 22,346 frames. We further introduce a Cross-Medium Recovery Policy (CMRP) centered on confidence-triggered template selection. It supplies MixFormerV2 with the fixed initial template, a window-best pre-trigger template, and a trigger-frame Kalman-guided image crop, together with their associated weights, without retraining the visual backbone. In the accuracy evaluation, CMRP achieves 49.90 Macro Success AUC, 2.95 points above MixFormerV2 Official. On selected cross-medium transition and occlusion-recovery intervals, CMRP increases MixFormerV2 tracking coverage from 47.91\% to 50.43\% relative to Official updating, while mean loss-to-recovery latency over successfully recovered videos decreases from 55.3 to 49.3 frames.

</details>


### [35] [Skeleton-Guided Progressive Test-Time Adaptation for Thin Curvilinear Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11104)
*Boa Jang,JunGyu Lee,Gwanho Lee,Jinwook Choi,Young-Gon Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SGP-TTA方法，通过渐进式批归一化和骨架一致性召回，在跨模态域偏移下实现细长曲线结构分割的拓扑连通性保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细长曲线结构（如血管、道路）的分割具有结构脆弱性：极小的像素级错误即可破坏全局拓扑，而在跨模态域偏移（源域与目标域成像过程根本不同）下这种脆弱性导致灾难性失败。现有测试时自适应方法仅调整特征统计量和置信度，未约束连通性，因此在极端域间隙下性能退化。

Method: 提出骨架引导的渐进式测试时自适应（SGP-TTA），包含两个核心组件：1）渐进式批归一化（ProgBN）：通过样本计数调度，将归一化从冻结的源域统计量逐步向当前目标域估计值迁移，使源-目标平衡随自适应阶段变化，而非固定系数；2）一致性骨架召回（CSR）：从几何对齐的多视图预测中导出结构目标，仅更新BN仿射参数以保留连通结构。

Result: 大量实验表明，SGP-TTA在拓扑连通性上持续优于现有TTA方法，且在跨模态偏移场景下取得最大领先优势。

Conclusion: SGP-TTA通过将归一化统计的自适应与结构约束相结合，有效解决了跨模态域偏移下细长曲线结构分割的拓扑断裂问题，为无源测试时自适应提供了新思路。

Abstract: Accurate segmentation of thin curvilinear structures is vital for various real-world applications, from vessel analysis to road extraction. Yet their intricate geometry makes even minor pixel-wise errors enough to break the global topology, and this structural fragility turns severe domain shifts into catastrophic failures. The difficulty is most acute under cross-modality gaps, where the imaging process itself differs fundamentally between source and target. While test-time adaptation (TTA) offers a practical source-free remedy, existing methods adapt feature statistics and confidence, neither of which constrains connectivity, and thus degrade under such extreme gaps. To address this, we propose Skeleton-Guided Progressive Test-Time Adaptation (SGP-TTA). Progressive Batch Normalization (ProgBN) shifts normalization from frozen source statistics toward current target estimates under a sample-count schedule, so that the source-target balance follows the stage of adaptation rather than a fixed coefficient. Consensus Skeleton Recall (CSR) then derives a structural target from geometrically aligned multi-view predictions and updates only the BN affine parameters to preserve connected structures. Extensive experiments show that SGP-TTA consistently outperforms existing TTA methods in topological connectivity, with the largest margins under cross-modality shift. The project page is available at https://boa-jang.github.io/SGP-TTA.

</details>


### [36] [TKCAM: Text and Keyframe to Camera Trajectory Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11105)
*Haozhe Yang,Zhiyang Dou,Zekai Gu,Cheng Lin,Wenping Wang,Yuan Liu,Taku Komura*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TKCAM，一个基于生成式掩码建模的文本与关键帧条件相机运动合成框架，通过12维运动学特征、RVQ分层标记与两阶段掩码Transformer实现可控相机轨迹生成，并构建RealEstate10K-Cap数据集与跨域评测基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量、可控的相机运动生成对AI辅助电影摄影、视频合成和3D场景理解至关重要。然而现有方法在多模态条件（文本与稀疏视觉观测）下生成连贯中间轨迹的能力不足，且缺乏统一的评测标准与大规模文本-相机数据集。

Method: 1) 用12维运动学特征（位置、速度、连续旋转表示）刻画相机动态，并通过残差向量量化器（RVQ）离散化为分层运动标记；2) 采用两阶段掩码Transformer架构重建与细化标记，通过显式自注意力与交叉注意力模块实现多模态条件融合；3) 支持稀疏视觉关键帧条件：用户提供自由文本提示与若干时间戳的RGB观测，作为时间局部化的视觉引导以生成连贯的中间轨迹。

Result: 在Fréchet距离（FID）、文本-运动匹配分数与检索指标（R@K）上超越近期SOTA基线；额外分析评估了时间平滑性与跨域泛化能力。同时构建了大规模文本-相机数据集RealEstate10K-Cap，并建立了基于Universal CLaTr Evaluator的跨域基准。代码已开源。

Conclusion: TKCAM通过分层运动标记与掩码Transformer结合稀疏关键帧条件，实现了高质量、可控且连贯的相机运动合成，并配套数据集与评测基准推动该领域标准化评估。

Abstract: Generating high-quality and controllable camera motion is essential for AI-assisted cinematography, video synthesis, and 3D scene understanding. We introduce TKCAM, a Text- and Keyframe-conditioned CAMera-motion synthesis framework based on generative masked modeling. We represent camera dynamics using a 12-dimensional kinematic feature comprising position, velocity, and a continuous rotation representation and discretize them into hierarchical motion tokens via a Residual Vector Quantizer (RVQ). A two-stage masked transformer architecture then learns to reconstruct and refine these tokens, utilizing explicit self- and cross-attention modules for multimodal conditioning. A central feature of our framework is sparse visual keyframe conditioning: users can provide free-form text prompts together with RGB observations at selected timestamps, which provide temporally localized visual guidance for generating coherent in-between trajectories. Furthermore, to advance evaluation standards, we curate RealEstate10K-Cap, a large-scale text-camera dataset, and establish a cross-domain benchmark with a Universal CLaTr Evaluator. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TKCAM surpasses recent state-of-the-art baselines on Fréchet distance (FID), text-motion matching scores, and retrieval metrics (R@K), while additional analyses evaluate temporal smoothness and cross-domain generalization. Code is available at https://github.com/linearalgebrayhz/TKCAM.

</details>


### [37] [DiscoVL: Unveiling Disentangled C ross-Modal Representation Learning via Orthogonal Adversarial Regularization for V ision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11113)
*Mengping Dong,Jinbao Li,Fei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DiscoVL：解耦跨模态表示学习框架，结合正交对抗正则与结构化跨模态对齐，通过多分支低秩残差对齐器与正交对抗三元组损失，提升视觉-语言模型在基类到新类泛化、跨数据集评估和少样本学习中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练视觉-语言模型感知能力强，但适配新下游任务时难以兼顾泛化性。现有参数高效提示学习方法常产生不一致表示，且未考虑语义分布偏移。此外，跨模态交互不足，传统三元组约束易使特征过拟合到类质心，导致泛化下降。

Method: 提出DiscoVL解耦跨模态表示学习框架，将正交对抗正则化与结构化跨模态对齐耦合。具体包含：多分支低秩残差对齐器，将表示分解为子空间，并在每层实现视觉与文本流的双向跨模态反馈；针对三元组损失设计正交正则化，防止质心坍塌，增强泛化。

Result: 在15个基准上评估，DiscoVL在基类到新类泛化、跨数据集评估和少样本学习三类任务上均相较当前最先进方法取得一致提升。

Conclusion: DiscoVL通过解耦表示与正交对抗正则，有效缓解跨模态交互不足与质心坍塌问题，为视觉-语言模型的高效适配与泛化提供了一种有效方案。

Abstract: Pre-trained vision-language models excel across varied perception tasks, but adapting them to novel downstream settings without sacrificing generalization remains non-trivial. Existing parameter-efficient prompt learning method often yields inconsistent representations and fails to account for semantic distribution shifts. In this work, we present DiscoVL, a disentangled cross-modal representation learning framework that couples orthogonal adversarial regularization with structured cross-modal alignment for vision-language models. To address the insufficient cross-modal interaction, our DiscoVL designs a multi-branch low-rank residual aligner that decomposes representations into subspaces and enables bidirectional cross-modal feedback between visual and textual streams at each layer. Furthermore, while conventional triplet constraints overfit features to class centroids, we design an orthogonal regularization for adversarial triplet loss, which prevents centroid collapse and substantially boosts generalization. Evaluations on 15 benchmarks demonstrate that DiscoVL delivers consistent improvements over state-of-the-art methods for base-to-novel generalization, cross-dataset evaluation, and few-shot learning

</details>


### [38] [MCL: Meta Convolution Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11117)
*Naim Reza,Md Al Amin,Ho Yub Jung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Meta Convolution Layer (MCL)，从函数视角将卷积核建模为输入条件的高阶多项式函数W(x)，用嵌套残差块实现多项式元网络，生成单一自适应核，解耦表达力与混合核数量，可即插即用集成CNN与Transformer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态卷积将有效核表示为少量基核的线性混合，表达力受限，且随混合规模增大优化变得复杂、训练不稳定。作者希望从函数角度重新审视动态卷积，摆脱混合核数量的限制。

Method: 提出MCL，将卷积核直接建模为输入条件函数W(x)，通过高阶多项式展开实现；借鉴深度多项式网络，用嵌套残差块构建结构化的多项式元网络，生成单个输入自适应核。MCL为即插件，可与标准卷积配合，嵌入CNN和Transformer骨干网络。

Result: 在ImageNet上，加入MCL使ResNet-18、ResNet-50、ResNet-101的Top-1准确率分别提升6.61%、3.42%、3.05%；在CIFAR-10/100上显著提升ResNet与Wide-ResNet变体精度；在细粒度视觉分类任务上以Swin和ViT为骨干优于既有方法。

Conclusion: 高阶多项式核生成是线性混合式动态卷积的强大且可扩展的替代方案，能在多种骨干与任务上稳定提升性能。

Abstract: Dynamic convolution enhances convolutional neural networks (CNNs) by adapting kernels to input content, but it expresses the effective kernel as a linear mixture of a small number of basis kernels, which limits expressivity and complicates optimization as the mixture size grows. In this work, we revisit dynamic convolution from a functional perspective and propose the Meta Convolution Layer (MCL), which directly models the convolutional kernel as an input-conditioned function W(x) realized via a high-order polynomial expansion. Leveraging nested residual blocks inspired by deep polynomial networks, MCL implements a structured polynomial meta-network that generates a single input-adaptive kernel, thereby decoupling representational power from the explicit number of mixture kernels and alleviating training instability. MCL is a plug-in addition with standard convolutions and can be seamlessly integrated into both CNN and transformer backbones. Experimental evaluation shows that adding MCL improves the Top-1 accuracy of Resnet- 18, Resnet-50 and ResNet-101 by 6.61%, 3.42% and 3.05% on the ImageNet dataset. Moreover, the proposed method significantly boosts the accuracy of Resnet and Wide-Resnet variants on CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100 datasets. Additionally, the proposed method outperforms previous methods on fine-grained visual classification tasks using Swin and ViT backbones. These results demonstrate that high-order polynomial kernel generation is a powerful and scalable alternative to linear mixture based dynamic convolution.

</details>


### [39] [IntrinSync: Joint Intrinsic Decomposition and Reciprocal Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11138)
*Zheng Gu,Rui Huang,Xilu Zhang,Jingbo Zhang,Min Lu,Zhida Sun,Dani Lischinski,Daniel Cohen-Or,Hui Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一逆渲染框架IntrinSync，通过联合通道建模与逆-正渲染互惠机制，实现内在属性的一致分解与图像编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有逆渲染方法要么孤立建模各内在通道，要么将逆渲染与正渲染视为独立过程，未能充分利用外观、光照、几何与材质之间的相互依赖，导致分解结果虽各自合理却难以相互兼容地解释原图。

Method: 通道层面：采用1对N映射，将输入RGB联合分解为反照率、阴影、表面法线、粗糙度和金属度，使各通道在生成过程中持续交换信息。过程层面：构建双循环一致性目标，形成逆渲染与正渲染的闭环互惠约束，对齐环内对应预测。

Result: 在三个数据集上取得有竞争力的内在属性估计与正渲染性能，提升了分解的连贯性与物理一致性；并提供物理可靠的图像编辑接口，可显式操控内在属性并渲染回RGB图像。

Conclusion: 联合通道建模与逆-正渲染互惠是实现内在属性相互兼容分解的有效途径，同时为基于物理的图像编辑提供了可操作的接口。

Abstract: Inverse rendering decomposes an image into intrinsic properties such as appearance, illumination, geometry, and material, yet these properties are inherently interdependent. A reliable decomposition should produce intrinsic maps that are not only individually plausible, but also mutually compatible in explaining the image. However, existing methods either model intrinsic channels in isolation or treat inverse and forward rendering as separate processes, leaving the interdependence underexploited. In this paper, we introduce IntrinSync, a unified framework that captures this interdependence through joint-channel modeling and reciprocal inverse-forward rendering. At the channel level, we jointly decompose an input RGB into albedo, shading, surface normal, roughness, and metallic maps through a 1-to-N mapping, enabling information exchange across channels throughout generation. At the process level, we establish inverse-forward reciprocity through a dual cycle-consistent objective that aligns corresponding predictions across a closed loop. Experiments on three datasets demonstrate that our method achieves competitive intrinsic estimation and forward rendering performance, improving coherence and physical consistency. Beyond decomposition, IntrinSync provides a physically grounded interface for image editing, allowing intrinsic properties to be explicitly manipulated and rendered back into RGB images.

</details>


### [40] [Improving Image-Based Nutrition Estimation Through Multimodal Food-Item Verification and Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11144)
*Jingbo Yue,Bruce Coburn,Jinge Ma,Jui-Feng Chi,Fengqing Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需任务微调的MLLM框架，通过食物清单盘点、身份与区域可行性双重验证、单次定向补全与再验证，实现单图营养估计的可靠化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单图营养估计存在"静默失败"：可见食物被遗漏时系统不会报错；即使食物识别正确，其候选区域也未必支持份量估计。现有方法缺乏对遗漏与区域可用性的显式校验。

Method: 利用多模态大语言模型：(1) 盘点可见食物；(2) 分别验证食物身份与每个候选2D区域是否支持份量估计；(3) 通过一次全图复核整合验证结果，识别未解决的缺口与被遗漏食物；(4) 触发至多一次定向补全；(5) 对补全区域在不提供补全提示的条件下重新验证，并归并为最终食物项集合用于营养估计。全流程无需任务特定微调。

Result: 在常见有效输出样本上的匹配评估显示：项目级定位（grounding）在所有测试设置下均提升质量估计精度，相对适配的检索基线提升能量估计精度，同时提升食物身份识别的精确率与召回率；补全后的视觉覆盖率在推理时单独评估，无需真值标注。

Conclusion: 通过MLLM驱动的验证—复核—补全闭环，可在零微调条件下缓解单图营养估计的静默失败，提升定位、质量与能量估计及身份识别性能，并为补全覆盖率提供无需标注的自评机制。

Abstract: Single-image nutrition estimation can fail silently when visible foods are missed. Even when a food is correctly identified, its proposed region may not support portion estimation. We propose a framework that uses multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to inventory visible foods and separately verify food identity and whether each proposed 2D region supports portion estimation. One whole-image review uses these verification results to identify unresolved gaps and omitted foods, triggering at most one targeted recovery pass. Recovered regions are re-verified without access to the recovery prompt, then reconciled into a final item set for nutrition estimation. The framework requires no task-specific fine-tuning. Matched evaluation on common valid-output samples shows that item-level grounding improves mass accuracy across all tested settings and energy accuracy relative to an adapted retrieval baseline, with item-identity precision and recall also improving, while post-recovery visual coverage is assessed separately at inference time without ground-truth annotations.

</details>


### [41] [SP-DocReader: Difference-Aware Self-Play for Precise Document OCR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11148)
*Wenjie Liao,Xiaohui Song,Liangjie Zhao,Haonan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SP-DocReader自博弈OCR框架，通过阅读差异掩码与聚焦保真损失，在冻结主干下针对SFT后残差错误训练，显著降低字符错误率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限输入与训练预算下，视觉语言模型仍难以实现精确的页面转录。监督微调（SFT）后仍存在残差错误，需要一种聚焦于这些残余错误的训练方法来进一步提升OCR精度。

Method: SP-DocReader自博弈框架。包含两个核心机制：Reading Discrepancy Masking，通过最长公共子序列（LCS）对齐参考与生成模型的token，并用完整的条件前缀对未匹配位置打分；Focused Fidelity Loss，在未匹配的ground-truth位置施加直接的负对数似然监督。仅训练OCR模块，主干网络保持冻结；并推导联合梯度以区分相对分数优化与直接监督。

Result: 与SFT-2相比，SP-DR-3在两个主干上均降低Vary-600K字符错误率。在Qwen3-VL-4B上，字符错误率降低约54%，DocVQA的ANLS提升3.7个点。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将自博弈训练聚焦于监督微调后仍存在的差异具有显著价值。

Abstract: Accurate page transcription remains difficult for vision language models under limited input and training budgets. We present SP-DocReader, a self-play framework for optical character recognition (OCR) that targets residual errors after supervised fine-tuning. Reading Discrepancy Masking aligns reference and generated model tokens through a longest common subsequence, then scores unmatched positions with their full conditioning prefixes. Focused Fidelity Loss adds direct negative log-likelihood supervision at unmatched ground-truth positions. Only the OCR module is trained, while the backbone remains frozen. We derive the combined gradient to distinguish relative score optimization from direct supervision. Compared with SFT-2, SP-DR-3 reduces Vary-600K character error rate on both backbones. On Qwen3-VL-4B, it reduces character error rate by approximately 54 percent and improves DocVQA Average Normalized Levenshtein Similarity (ANLS) by 3.7 points. These results show the value of focusing self-play training on the discrepancies that remain after supervised fine-tuning.

</details>


### [42] [A Unified Score Matching Paradigm for Video Anomaly Detection and Anticipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11149)
*Congqi Cao,Zhenhe Liang,Hanwen Zhang,Yifan Zhao,Qinyi Lv,Lingtong Min,Yanning Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出统一分数驱动框架 Uni-DSM，基于去噪分数匹配（DSM）同时建模视频异常检测（VAD）与视频异常预期（VAA），通过共享噪声条件分数 Transformer 主干及不同推理/监督范式，在多个基准上取得最优性能并保持高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式 VAD 方法虽从分布视角检测异常，但受限于局部异常模式；而 VAA 作为超越事后检测的主动扩展，面临更大挑战，尤其是 VAD 中依赖真实帧的对比推理范式不适用于 VAA，阻碍其发展。因此需要统一且可扩展的框架同时支持检测与预期。

Method: 提出 Uni-DSM 统一分数驱动框架，基于去噪分数匹配，通过似然估计与分数函数在学习到的数据分布上建模异常模式。采用共享的噪声条件分数 Transformer 主干，结合场景相关嵌入与运动感知加权进行分布级建模。VAD 实例化为自回归去噪分数匹配（ADSM），通过自回归去噪逐步累积异常证据；VAA 复用同一架构并引入轻量辅助解码器与自蒸馏去噪分数匹配（SDSM），利用输出差异构建监督以替代不可得的未来真实帧。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在 VAD 和 VAA 任务上均达到最先进性能，同时保持高效率。

Conclusion: Uni-DSM 建立了从异常检测到异常预期的统一且可扩展的流水线，验证了基于分数的一体化建模在主动安全任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Video anomaly detection (VAD) is a fundamental and safety-critical task in computer vision. Recent generative approaches detect anomalies from a distributional perspective, but remain limited by local anomaly modes. Meanwhile, video anomaly anticipation (VAA), as a proactive extension beyond post-hoc detection, introduces additional challenges. In particular, the contrastive inference paradigm in VAD, which relies on ground-truth frames, is not applicable to VAA, hindering its development. To address these challenges, we propose a unified score-driven framework, termed Uni-DSM, based on denoising score matching (DSM), which models anomaly patterns through likelihood estimation and score functions over the learned data distribution. Within this unified framework, we adopt a shared noise-conditioned score transformer backbone with scene-dependent embeddings and motion-aware weighting for distribution-level modeling. Instead of introducing separate architectures, Uni-DSM unifies VAD and VAA through different inference and supervision paradigms built upon the same score-based formulation. For VAD, we instantiate an autoregressive denoising score matching (ADSM) mechanism, which progressively accumulates anomalous evidence via autoregressive denoising, enabling enhanced perception of local modes beyond visual cues. For VAA, we extend the same architecture by incorporating a lightweight auxiliary decoder and a novel self-distilled denoising score matching (SDSM) mechanism. By constructing supervision from output discrepancies instead of relying on unavailable future ground truth, our method achieves efficient training suitable or early anomaly anticipation. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate state-of-the-art performance in both VAD and VAA while maintaining high efficiency, establishing a unified and scalable pipeline from anomaly detection to anticipation.

</details>


### [43] [CARE: Constrained Attention Refinement for Fine-Grained Visual Classification via Teacher-Student Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11153)
*Ruibo Wen,Hang Shao,Yiming Lei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CARE，一种基于教师-学生蒸馏的约束注意力精炼框架，用于可解释细粒度视觉分类，在冻结骨干的可解释设定下取得较强分类与解释忠实度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度视觉分类既需识别细微局部特征，又需给出预测依据。类特定注意力路径天然可解释，但其受限的预测结构削弱判别能力，且未充分利用强预训练骨干的中间层表示。

Method: CARE将最终预测与解释保留在类特定注意力学生网络中，引入仅在训练时使用的辅助查询教师：教师以可学习查询读取DINOv2选定的中间层，融合多层级表示，并将logit标准化的类判别知识迁移给学生；同时设计多样性与稀疏正则项约束学生注意力头，减少冗余、促进紧凑的性状定位。

Result: 在CUB、Oxford-IIIT Pet、Stanford Dogs和Stanford Cars上，CARE在可解释的冻结骨干设定下取得较强分类性能，CUB上Top-1达78.5%；插入/删除忠实性分析表明排名靠前的注意力区域保留了类别相关证据。

Conclusion: 通过训练期教师蒸馏与注意力正则化，可在保持类特定注意力可解释路径的同时提升细粒度识别性能与解释忠实度，验证了利用冻结强骨干中间表示进行可解释分类的可行性。

Abstract: Fine-grained visual classification requires models to recognize subtle local traits while exposing the visual evidence behind their predictions. Class-specific attention pathways provide a natural basis for interpretable recognition, but their constrained prediction structure limits discriminative capacity and underuses intermediate representations from strong pretrained backbones. To address this problem, we propose CARE, a constrained attention refinement framework for interpretable fine-grained recognition via teacher-student distillation. CARE keeps the final prediction and explanation within a class-specific attention student, while introducing a training-only auxiliary query teacher that reads selected intermediate DINOv2 layers with learnable queries. The teacher fuses multi-level representations and transfers logit-standardized class-discriminative knowledge to the student. To further refine the explanation pathway, we design diversity and sparsity terms to regularize student attention heads, reducing redundancy and encouraging compact trait localization. Experiments on CUB, Oxford-IIIT Pet, Stanford Dogs, and Stanford Cars show that CARE achieves strong classification performance under an interpretable frozen-backbone setting, reaching 78.5% Top-1 accuracy on CUB. Faithfulness analysis with insertion and deletion metrics further indicates that the top-ranked attention regions retain class-relevant evidence for explanation.

</details>


### [44] [VGGTWorld-VLA: Intent-Conditioned 3D World Evolution for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11161)
*Zhaoyang Liu,Kun Jiang,Ziying Song,Diange Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 VGGTWorld-VLA，在 VGGT-World 基础上引入意图/动作条件，实现自动驾驶中可控的 3D 世界演化预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VGGT 类扩展虽能进行时序 3D 预测，但其未来演化对驾驶意图与动作的依赖较弱，难以建模同一场景在不同自车动作下的多种未来。

Method: 1) 动作—语义条件机制：将驾驶语义与自车运动表征注入未来 token 流，使同一观测在不同动作下产生不同未来几何；2) 几何—语言—动作桥接：融合历史几何、VLA 语义特征以及机动与轨迹表征，联合条件化未来几何预测。

Result: 在 NAVSIM 上评估未来几何预测，与基线相比取得具有竞争力的性能；条件消融实验验证了语义与动作条件的有效性。

Conclusion: 语义与动作条件化对基于 VGGT 的可控世界预测具有潜力。

Abstract: VGGT provides a strong foundation for geometry-centric world models by recovering unified 3D scene geometry from visual observations. Although recent extensions enable temporal 3D prediction, their future evolution remains weakly conditioned on driving intentions and actions, limiting their ability to model alternative action-dependent futures. We propose VGGTWorld-VLA, an intention-conditioned extension of VGGT-World for controllable 3D world evolution in autonomous driving. First, we introduce an action--semantic conditioning mechanism that injects complementary driving semantics and ego-motion representations into the future-token stream, enabling different future geometry predictions for the same observed scene under alternative ego actions. Second, we develop a geometry--language--action bridge that adapts historical geometry, VLA semantic features, and maneuver and trajectory representations for joint conditioning of future geometry prediction. We evaluate future geometry prediction on NAVSIM, while conditioning ablations further examine the contributions of semantic and action information. Compared with the baseline, our method demonstrates competitive geometry prediction performance. Ablation studies further support the effectiveness of semantic and action conditioning. These results demonstrate the potential of semantic and action conditioning for controllable VGGT-based world prediction in autonomous driving.

</details>


### [45] [AutoAdapt: Reliable Few-Shot Adaptation under Clinical Distribution Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11162)
*Song Wang,Jie Peng,Davis Hobley,Zachary Plotkin,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 AutoAdapt 框架，通过 Adapter（可扩展的适配配方空间）与 Automator（基于患者证据的加权配方组合）两组件，配合可靠性准则，解决小样本跨医院适配中策略选择难与评估不可靠的问题，在多个临床数据集上取得 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型预训练临床模型可通过适配复用先验知识，但目标医院往往仅有少量标注患者（few-shot adaptation）。此时需决策适配哪个模型、更新多少参数、用哪些患者，面临两大挑战：最优适配策略因临床任务而异；小队列使候选策略的评估与比较不可靠。

Method: 提出 AutoAdapt，包含两个核心设计：Adapter 定义可扩展的适配配方（recipe）空间；Automator 基于适配患者内部的证据形成加权配方组合。并引入可靠性准则，确保只选择在大多数可用患者上最有效的策略，进而组合成适配方案。

Result: 在重症监护、急诊护理及诊断数据集上开展大量实验，AutoAdapt 仅用少量患者进行适配即可持续取得最先进（SOTA）性能。

Conclusion: AutoAdapt 通过配方空间建模与基于证据的加权组合，并以可靠性准则约束选择，有效缓解了任务异质性与小样本评估不可靠的问题，验证了其在真实临床小样本适配场景中的实用性与优越性。

Abstract: Large pretrained clinical models provide a practical way to reuse learned prior knowledge across hospitals by adapting models to them. In practice, a target hospital may only have a small labeled patient cohort, a setting commonly referred to as few-shot adaptation. This requires making multiple decisions, such as which pretrained model to adapt, how much of the model to update, and which patients to use. Nevertheless, this process faces two primary challenges. First, the best adaptation strategy varies across clinical tasks. Second, evaluating and comparing candidate strategies becomes unreliable due to the small patient cohort. In this work, we introduce AutoAdapt with two core designs to deal with these challenges. The Adapter defines an extensible space of adaptation recipes, and the Automator forms a weighted recipe combination from evidence within the adaptation patients. We propose a reliability rule to ensure that only the most effective strategy on most available patients will be selected. These selected strategies then form a combination for effective few-shot adaptation. We conduct extensive experiments across critical care, emergency care, and diagnostic datasets, and the results show that AutoAdapt consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance using only a few patients for adaptation.

</details>


### [46] [VAMR: Multi-Question Agentic Reasoning for Efficient Long-Form Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11171)
*Runquan Gui,Hanzhu Chen,Zehao Wang,Hanxin Zhu,Xin Li,Zhibo Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VAMR（Video Agent for Multi-Question Reasoning），通过单一共享工具使用轨迹协调同一视频上的多个问题，并结合问题条件视觉感知、分层多问题记忆与问题视野策略优化（QHPO），在多个长视频理解基准上取得最高准确率与最少推理轮数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频智能体对同一长视频中的每个问题独立执行工具使用轨迹，反复重启视频探索与记忆构建，无法联合获取证据并逐步建立支持整个问题集的共享理解，导致效率低、冗余高。

Method: 1) VAMR在单条共享轨迹中协调所有问题，每轮由持久策略模型为一个或多个未解决问题调用工具，并对证据充分的问题提交答案；2) 问题条件视觉感知在一次调用中为多个问题检索细粒度线索；3) 分层多问题记忆将可复用上下文整合为共享视频故事，同时保留各问题的独立证据；4) 先用监督微调初始化交互协议，再提出问题视野策略优化（QHPO）：问题级critic估计各活跃问题的价值，轮次对齐将问题优势映射到直接服务该问题的轮次，聚合并优化共享actor。

Result: 在LVBench、Video-Holmes和LongVideoBench上，VAMR总体准确率最高，且在迭代方法中推理轮数最少。在LVBench上达到62.1%准确率，超过VideoARM 4.3个百分点，同时推理轮数与处理帧数分别减少85.9%与61.4%。

Conclusion: 通过共享工具使用轨迹与多问题联合记忆，VAMR能协同、渐进地构建统一视频理解，在保持甚至提升准确率的同时显著降低推理轮数和帧处理量，为长视频多问题推理提供了高效范式。

Abstract: Long-form video understanding often involves multiple questions about different aspects of the same recording. Yet existing video agents typically process each question through an isolated tool-use trajectory. This repeatedly restarts video exploration and memory construction, missing opportunities to acquire evidence jointly and progressively build a shared understanding that supports the complete question set. We introduce \textbf{VAMR} (\textbf{V}ideo \textbf{A}gent for \textbf{M}ulti-Question \textbf{R}easoning), which coordinates all questions about a video through one shared tool-use trajectory. At each round, a persistent policy model can invoke tools for one or more unresolved questions and submit answers for questions with sufficient evidence. Question-conditioned visual perception retrieves fine-grained clues for several questions in one call, while layered multi-question memory integrates reusable context into a shared video story and preserves separate evidence for individual questions. After supervised fine-tuning initializes this interaction protocol, we propose question-horizon policy optimization (\qhpo) to optimize shared trajectories in which questions progress and finish at different rounds. Specifically, a question-level critic estimates the value of each active question, while round alignment maps each question advantage to the rounds that directly serve it before the aligned advantages are aggregated to optimize the shared actor. Across LVBench, Video-Holmes, and LongVideoBench, VAMR achieves the highest accuracy overall and the fewest reasoning rounds among iterative methods. On LVBench, it reaches 62.1\% accuracy, exceeding VideoARM by \textbf{4.3} points while reducing reasoning rounds and processed frames by \textbf{85.9\%} and \textbf{61.4\%}.

</details>


### [47] [IntactWorld: Joint World Modeling with Intact Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11174)
*Boming Tan,Xiangdong Zhang,Yan Xia,Qi Zhu,Deyi Ji,Xue Yang,Shaofeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 IntactWorld，一种基于无压缩完整特征的联合世界建模架构，通过预测中间层干净特征 x0 消除流匹配的流形间隙，并采用 Full-to-Compact 训练范式降低开销，在 VBench 2.0 上超过基线 2.46 分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型虽能生成逼真画面，却缺乏对真实世界内在逻辑的理解。已有方法通过内化世界知识来理解世界，但受计算开销或维度对齐限制，学习过程不可避免地压缩特征，导致结构信息严重丢失。

Method: 1) IntactWorld：联合世界建模架构，使用无压缩的完整特征（Intact Features）；2) 针对高维无压缩空间中预测流速度 v 造成的流形间隙，改为预测中间层的干净特征 x0；3) Full-to-Compact 训练范式：用高度精炼的 CLS token 替代原始完整特征，实现高效单分支引导。

Result: 空间内存消耗降低 11.4%，推理延迟降低 43.8%；在 VBench 2.0 基准上以 2.46 分的优势超越现有强基线。

Conclusion: IntactWorld 通过保留完整特征并改进预测目标，兼顾世界知识建模的完整性与计算效率，实现了优于现有基线的视频生成与世界理解性能。

Abstract: While recent video generation models synthesize highly realistic visuals, they lack a genuine understanding of intrinsic real-world logic. Existing methods attempt to understand the world by internalizing diverse world knowledge, yet constrained by computational overhead or dimensionality alignment, their learning processes inevitably compress features, causing a severe loss of structural information. To address this, we propose \textbf{IntactWorld}, a \textbf{Joint World Modeling Architecture} utilizing uncompressed \textbf{Intact Features}. Since data naturally reside on a low-dimensional manifold within a high-dimensional space, predicting the flow velocity $v$ within this uncompressed high-dimensional space induces a severe manifold gap. To successfully eliminate this optimization bottleneck, our framework instead predicts the clean feature $x_0$ at intermediate layers. Furthermore, to mitigate the computational overhead of incorporating complete world knowledge, we introduce a \textit{Full-to-Compact Training Paradigm}. By replacing raw full features with highly refined CLS tokens, this paradigm enables efficient single-branch guidance, reducing spatial memory consumption by 11.4\% and cutting inference latency by 43.8\%. Extensive evaluations demonstrate the effectiveness of IntactWorld, outperforming established baselines by 2.46 points on the VBench 2.0 benchmark.

</details>


### [48] [Multimodal Remote Sensing Image Registration: A Comprehensive Review, Challenges and Prospects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11176)
*Zhiqiang Han,Yuanxin Ye,Qiuyun Wu,Jinhao Chen,Bai Zhu,Siyuan Hao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统综述多模态遥感图像配准方法，按配准流程分为区域法、特征法和深度学习法三类，梳理各类核心原理、代表性算法及优缺点，总结公开数据集，并指出当前瓶颈与未来趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态遥感图像配准是图像融合、变化检测、目标识别等协同处理与下游应用的关键前提。但多模态图像间存在辐射、几何、尺度、视角和时间等显著差异，这些差异由传感器几何、物理辐射机制、成像平台和环境扰动等因素驱动，给高精度、鲁棒配准带来严峻挑战，因此亟需对该领域进展进行系统梳理。

Method: 综述性研究。依据配准流程将现有多模态遥感图像配准方法划分为三类：基于区域的方法、基于特征的方法和基于深度学习的方法；逐一详述各类别的核心原理、代表性算法、优势与局限；并归纳遥感领域公开的多模态图像数据集，分析其特性与适用场景。

Result: 完成了主流多模态遥感图像配准方法的系统分类与对比分析，明确了区域法、特征法、深度学习法三类方法的原理、代表算法及优缺点；整理并分析了公开多模态遥感数据集的特性与适用场景，为方法评估提供数据支撑。

Conclusion: 指出当前高精度配准研究面临的瓶颈问题，并展望未来发展趋势，旨在为相关领域研究者提供全面参考和有价值的见解。

Abstract: Multimodal remote sensing image registration is a crucial prerequisite for the collaborative processing and downstream application of remote sensing data, such as image fusion, change detection, and target recognition. However, significant variations in radiometry, geometry, scale, viewpoint, and time often exist between multimodal images. These differences, driven by varying sensor geometries, physical radiation mechanisms, imaging platforms, and environmental disturbances, pose severe challenges to achieving high-precision, robust registration. This paper systematically reviews the progress of mainstream multimodal remote sensing image registration methods. Based on their registration pipelines, existing approaches are categorized into three main types: region-based, feature-based, and deep learning-based methods. We detail the core principles, representative algorithms, advantages, and limitations of each category. Additionally, we summarize publicly available multimodal image datasets in the remote sensing domain, analyzing their specific characteristics and applicable scenarios. Finally, we highlight current bottlenecks in high-precision registration research and outline future development trends. This review aims to provide a comprehensive reference and valuable insights for researchers in related fields.

</details>


### [49] [MATE4D: Matrix-Guided Editable 4D Generation from a Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11181)
*Xiaotian Chen,Dongfu Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MATE4D 提出从单张图像生成可编辑动态4D内容的框架，通过构建时空多视图图像矩阵并优化3D高斯原语，结合轻量变形模块实现4D表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单张图像生成真实且时间稳定的4D内容仍具挑战性，因为单一视角提供的结构线索有限且运动证据较弱，导致几何、时间一致性和背景编辑存在问题。

Method: 构建带文本引导背景编辑的时空多视图图像矩阵，提供视角、外观与运动的连贯监督；用这些合成观测优化3D高斯原语，再通过轻量变形模块动画化，形成4D表示。

Result: 在 Objaverse-XL 和 Diffusion4D 数据集上，MATE4D 在视觉质量、效率和可控性上优于强基线，生成的场景几何更忠实、时间行为更平滑、背景编辑更一致，减少上下文歧义与运动伪影。

Conclusion: MATE4D 支持从单图像生成可编辑动态4D内容，具备实际AR/VR内容创作潜力。

Abstract: Generative models have rapidly pushed content creation be-yond 2D imagery toward dynamic 3D and 4D scene synthesis. Yet pro-ducing realistic and temporally stable 4D content from a single image is still difficult because one view provides limited structural cues and weak motion evidence. We introduce MATE4D, a framework that converts one input image into editable dynamic 4D content. Our method constructs a spatio-temporal multi-view image matrix with text-guided background manipulation, delivering coherent supervision over viewpoint, appear-ance, and motion. These synthesized observations are used to optimize 3D Gaussian primitives, which are then animated through a lightweight deformation module to form a 4D representation. The resulting scenes preserve geometry more faithfully, maintain smoother temporal behavior, and keep background edits more consistent, reducing context ambiguity and motion artifacts. Experiments on Objaverse-XL and Diffusion4D show that MATE4D outperforms strong baselines in visual quality, effi-ciency, and controllability, supporting practical AR/VR content creation.

</details>


### [50] [WorldFact-Bench: Beyond Image-Internal Plausibility to Image-World Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11184)
*Zhuohong Chen,Zhengxian Wu,Yunyao Yu,Hangrui Xu,Zijian Yu,Hao Tan,Zhifang Liu,Peng Jiao,Jun Lan,Haoqian Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 WorldFact-Bench 基准与 PERSIST-Agent 方法，从单张图像出发评估"图像-世界"事实一致性，用持续状态引导迭代验证，在 8B 骨干上超越直接判断与检索增强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像生成进步使视觉真实性难以判断。现有取证虽同时考察生成伪影与高层视觉不一致，但视觉上合理的图像仍可能与现实世界的事实或规则相矛盾；且已有评测通常依赖预设断言或验证目标，缺乏对单图独立进行世界事实一致性检验的设定。

Method: 1) 构建 WorldFact-Bench：含 1,274 组"源对齐"的真-假配对，覆盖四种验证范式与十个语义域；每对引入一个有证据支持的具体事实冲突，同时尽量保持非目标内容与视觉合理性。图像独立评估，配对准确率要求一对中两张均判对。2) 提出 PERSIST-Agent：以持续状态（persistent state）组织迭代验证，将候选事实、视觉观察、证据与验证状态相互关联；该状态指导后续检查与检索，并保留未决候选。3) 在固定骨干权重下，通过验证反馈对提示与执行规则进行 harness 自优化（self-optimization）。

Result: 实验揭示若干检测器存在明显标签偏置，检索带来的增益不均衡；在评测的 8B 骨干上，PERSIST-Agent 的配对准确率高于直接判断与检索增强基线，消融实验支持持续验证状态的作用。

Conclusion: 结果凸显状态引导验证的价值，并指出现有方法在"视觉合理性"与"事实正确性"之间仍存在未被弥合的差距。

Abstract: Advances in image generation have made visual authenticity increasingly difficult to assess. Although image forensics now examines both generation artifacts and higher-level visual inconsistencies, a plausible image can still contradict real-world facts or rules. We introduce WorldFact-Bench to evaluate image-world consistency from a single image, without a predefined claim or verification target. The benchmark contains 1,274 source-aligned real-fake pairs across four verification regimes and ten semantic domains. Each pair introduces a specific, evidence-supported factual conflict while seeking to preserve non-target content and visual plausibility. Images are evaluated independently, and pair accuracy requires both members of a pair to be classified correctly. We further propose PERSIST-Agent, which organizes iterative verification around a persistent state linking candidate facts, visual observations, evidence, and verification statuses. This state guides subsequent inspection and retrieval while retaining unresolved candidates. With backbone weights fixed, harness self-optimization refines the agent's prompts and execution rules through validation feedback. Experiments reveal strong label biases in several detectors and uneven gains from retrieval. On the evaluated 8B backbones, PERSIST-Agent improves pair accuracy over both direct judgment and retrieval-augmented baselines, while ablations support the role of persistent verification state. These findings highlight the value of state-guided verification and the remaining gap between visual plausibility and factual correctness.

</details>


### [51] [RGBD-to-3D Object Mesh Refinement via Depth Matching and Symmetry Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11187)
*Ahyun Seo,Minsu Cho*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量、即插即用的RGBD-to-3D精修方法，无需重训练即可提升任意RGB-to-3D重建器的单视图网格质量，各阶段均为闭式求解，速度快且精度优于现有精修方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单视图3D重建器常生成看似合理但与输入视图不一致的网格，尤其在深度不连续和自遮挡区域误差明显。现有测试时精修多依赖昂贵的优化，效率低。

Method: 给定深度图，先通过二部图匹配将可见表面校正到反投影深度点；再在检测到的对称平面两侧将校正镜像到遮挡面；最后用平滑求解器传播校正。所有阶段均为闭式解，可作为即插即用模块接入任意RGB-to-3D重建器，无需重训练。

Result: 在GSO和OmniObject3D数据集上结合五个骨干网络，精度一致提升；使用单目伪深度同样有效；对称物体上从对称性中获益更多；相比已有精修方法在精度和运行时间上均更优。还能改进RGB-D-to-mesh重建器，并迁移到含噪声传感器深度的真实拍摄场景。

Conclusion: 该闭式RGBD-to-3D精修框架通用、高效且无需重训练，可显著增强单视图重建与RGB-D重建的一致性与精度，并具备向真实场景迁移的能力。

Abstract: Single-view 3D reconstructors often produce plausible meshes that disagree with the input view, especially near depth discontinuities and self-occlusions. We present a lightweight, plug-and-play RGBD-to-3D refinement that improves any RGB-to-3D reconstructor without retraining. Given a depth map, we correct the visible surface by bipartite matching to back-projected depth points, mirror these corrections onto the occluded side across a detected symmetry plane, and propagate them with a smoothness solver. Every stage is closed-form, making the method orders of magnitude faster than optimization-heavy test-time refinement. On GSO and OmniObject3D with five backbones, it yields consistent gains, also with monocular pseudo-depth, benefits more from symmetry on symmetric objects, and compares favorably with prior refinement in accuracy and runtime. It further improves an RGB-D-to-mesh reconstructor and transfers to real captures with noisy sensor depth.

</details>


### [52] [3DTexMOR: 3D Gaussian Multi-Object Removal via Texture-Space Inpainting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11198)
*Kunxin Guang,Yonghao Zhao,Jian Yang,Beibei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出3DTexMOR，在统一纹理空间中进行补全以实现3D高斯场景的多目标移除，几何与外观补全效果优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于NeRF和3DGS的3D物体移除方法依赖2D图像修复来引导3D补全。但在多物体复杂布局下，各视角可见的周围上下文有限，导致2D修复易产生伪影；且不同视角补全结果不一致，带来相互冲突的监督信号与模糊重建。

Method: 核心思想是在所有视角共享的统一纹理空间中执行修复。具体做法：聚合多视角观测到纹理图，对缺失区域进行修复，再将补全后的纹理图重投影回相机视角以监督高斯场景补全；为规避视相关高光与反射影响，分解外观并聚合视无关的本征属性而非RGB颜色；此外引入几何正则化高斯补全以约束补全区域的几何。

Result: 大量实验表明补全结果视觉合理，多物体移除性能达到SOTA，相比现有方法PSNR提升5.8 dB，LPIPS至少降低22%。

Conclusion: 统一纹理空间补全通过融合互补观测提供更丰富上下文并促进跨视角外观一致性，是解决多目标3D移除中2D修复伪影与跨视角不一致问题的有效方案。

Abstract: 3D object removal aims to remove target objects from reconstructed scenes and complete the geometry and appearance of occluded regions. Existing NeRF- and 3DGS-based methods typically inpaint 2D images to guide 3D completion. However, complex multi-object layouts limit the surrounding context visible in each view, making 2D inpainting prone to artifacts. Inconsistent completions across views also introduce conflicting supervision and blurry reconstructions. We propose 3D Gaussian Multi-Object Removal via Texture-Space Inpainting (3DTexMOR). Our key idea is to perform inpainting in a unified texture space shared by all views. By combining complementary observations, this space provides richer context for recovering missing regions and promotes cross-view appearance consistency. We aggregate multi-view observations into texture maps, inpaint the missing regions, and reproject the completed maps into camera views to supervise Gaussian scene completion. To avoid the influence of view-dependent highlights and reflections, we decompose appearance and aggregate view-independent intrinsic attributes instead of RGB colors. We further introduce geometrically regularized Gaussian completion to constrain the geometry of the completed regions. Extensive experiments demonstrate visually plausible completions and state-of-the-art multi-object removal performance, improving PSNR by 5.8 dB and reducing LPIPS by at least 22% compared with existing methods.

</details>


### [53] [Dissecting Representation Structure in Vision Transformers: A Rigorous Architectural Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11205)
*Kim-Cuc Nguyen,Ngai-Man Cheung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文首次对ViT模块级特征信息进行严格分析，揭示其与泛化行为的关系，并据此提出高效的ViT设计指导，提出代理指标在相关性排序上提升18-48%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表征结构对理解ViT架构及其泛化行为至关重要，但既有研究既未隔离和分析模块级特征，也未探究其特征交互如何影响性能估计，因此需要系统性的模块级特征分析。

Method: 在多种架构尺度下对ViT特征信息进行严格分析：识别初始化时的特征塌陷并提出缓解方案；用熵和最小特征值量化特征信息；比较token空间与embedding空间特征；考察层内线性子模块特征的作用；并构建泛化预测代理指标。

Result: 发现初始化特征塌陷导致冗余；熵与最小特征值可作为泛化预测的可靠指标；token空间特征比embedding空间更忠实；ViT层内线性子模块产出的特征对泛化性能预测至关重要；所提代理指标较基线在相关性排序上提升18-48%，并能识别更优架构。

Conclusion: 模块级特征信息是理解ViT表征与泛化的关键，所提代理指标可有效预测泛化并指导在更低或相当计算成本下获得更高精度的ViT架构设计。

Abstract: Representation structure is crucial for understanding Vision Transformer (ViT) architectures and their generalization behavior. However, prior studies neither isolate nor analyze module-level features nor investigate how their interactions contribute to performance estimation. In this work, we conduct the first rigorous analysis of feature information across diverse architectural scales, empirically uncover the relationship between ViT representation and generalization behavior, and leverage these insights to guide efficient ViT design. Our contributions are fivefold: Across diverse architectural scales, 1) We identify feature collapse at initialization, which leads to redundancy, and propose a reduction scheme to mitigate this issue. 2) We quantify feature information using entropy and the minimum eigenvalue, demonstrating that these metrics serve as reliable indicators for generalization prediction. 3) We show that feature in the token space provides a more faithful representation than those in embedding space. 4) We discover an unexpected finding: features produced by linear submodules within ViT layers are critical for the prediction of generalization performance. 5) Our proposed proxy improves the correlation ranking by 18-48% over prior baselines and can effectively identify ViT architectures that achieve higher accuracy at lower or comparable computational cost.

</details>


### [54] [GATOR: Generative and Agentic 3D Object Reconstruction From Casual Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11215)
*Qirui Wu,Stan Birchfield,Hesam Rabeti,Angel X. Chang,Bowen Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GATOR是一个生成式与智能体协同的框架，能从一到多张随手拍摄的图像中重建带纹理的完整3D物体资产及其相对于场景的位姿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从随手图像重建完整且与场景对齐的3D物体，需要整合稀疏、不确定的观测，并推断被遮挡而不可见的表面，这是一个具有挑战性的问题。

Method: 该框架包含局部模态混合器，在跨视角推理前耦合patch对齐的RGB、目标掩膜与点图特征，从而保留场景上下文并区分目标与周围环境；通过阶段特定的适配器，以类别名称和物体描述作为文本引导的语义条件，分别服务于结构、几何与外观生成；生成的资产用于初始化一个多模态智能体，提供实例特定的几何与位姿，并通过"观测引导的编辑-渲染-审查"循环进行针对性的结构与纹理精修。

Result: 在合成物体、杂乱桌面和室内场景上，GATOR在几何与外观保真度方面表现优异，并能从稀疏观测中恢复场景相对位姿；时间预算对比与场景级仿真进一步验证了其重建效率与仿真可用性。

Conclusion: GATOR通过生成式先验与智能体式迭代精修的协同，实现了从稀疏随手图像到场景对齐、带纹理3D物体资产的高效高质量重建，并具备仿真应用潜力。

Abstract: Reconstructing complete, scene-aligned 3D objects from casual images requires integrating sparse, uncertain observations and inferring surfaces hidden by occlusions. We present GATOR, a generative and agentic framework that recovers textured object assets and their scene-relative pose from one or more images. Our local modality mixer couples patch-aligned RGB, target-mask, and pointmap features before cross-view reasoning, preserving scene context while distinguishing the target from its surroundings. Text-guided semantic conditioning complements these spatial cues with category names and object captions through stage-specific adapters for structure, geometry, and appearance generation. The generated asset initializes a multimodal agent, providing instance-specific geometry and pose for targeted structural and texture refinement through an observation-guided edit-render-review loop. Across synthetic objects, cluttered tabletops, and indoor scenes, GATOR achieves strong geometric and appearance fidelity while recovering scene-relative pose from sparse observations. Time-budget comparisons and scene-level simulation further demonstrate the reconstruction efficiency and simulation readiness. Project page: https://research.nvidia.com/labs/lpr/gator/

</details>


### [55] [Breaking the Group Size Barrier: Parameter-Efficient Group Dance Generation with Chain-of-Dancers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11237)
*Jing Xu,Cunjian Chen,Qiuhong Ke*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ChainDance将群舞生成重述为“Chain-of-Dancers”的逐舞者条件分布序列分解，基于冻结的单舞者扩散骨干并引入RATE与GAME两个轻量模块，实现可变群组规模、身份保持与较低训练成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群舞生成需同时建模舞者间密集依赖以保持空间协调，并保留各舞者个体身份。现有端到端Transformer联合建模所有舞者，导致架构绑定固定群组规模，且逐舞者身份在跨帧间被纠缠，难以扩展和保持身份。

Method: 提出ChainDance框架，将群舞生成重述为“Chain-of-Dancers”：对逐舞者条件分布做序列分解，使单一模型无需重训练即可适配可变群组规模并天然保持身份。方法构建在冻结的单舞者扩散骨干上，包含两个轻量模块：Role-Aware Text Encoder（RATE）提供逐舞者语义条件；Group-Aware Motion Encoder（GAME）通过距离加权图卷积网络聚合已生成舞者。推理时采用免训练噪声优化以增强全局空间一致性。

Result: 在AIOZ-GDance上，ChainDance取得最先进的运动质量与群组协调性，并在结构上保持逐舞者身份；相比先前方法参数减少3–4倍，训练时间减少3–6倍。

Conclusion: 通过序列化的Chain-of-Dancers分解与轻量条件/聚合模块，ChainDance在可变群组规模下实现高质量、协调且身份保持的群舞生成，同时显著降低参数与训练开销。

Abstract: Group dance generation aims to synthesize coordinated multi-dancer choreography from music, with broad applications in animation and interactive content creation. This task requires modeling dense inter-person dependencies to ensure spatial coordination, while naturally preserving individual dancer identities. Existing approaches model all dancers jointly with end-to-end transformers, which tie the architecture to a fixed group size and entangle per-dancer identities across frames. We propose ChainDance, a scalable framework that reformulates group dance generation as a Chain-of-Dancers: a sequential decomposition over per-dancer conditional distributions, allowing a single model to scale across variable group sizes without retraining and naturally preserving per-dancer identity. Built on a frozen single-dancer diffusion backbone, ChainDance introduces two lightweight modules: a Role-Aware Text Encoder (RATE) for per-dancer semantic conditioning, and a Group-Aware Motion Encoder (GAME) that aggregates previously generated dancers via a distance-weighted graph convolutional network, and incorporates a training-free noise optimization procedure at inference time to enforce global spatial coherence. Experiments on AIOZ-GDance demonstrate that ChainDance achieves state-of-the-art motion quality and group coordination while structurally preserving per-dancer identity, with $3$-$4\times$ fewer parameters and requiring $3$-$6\times$ less training time compared to prior approaches.

</details>


### [56] [Attributing HOW, Not Just WHICH: Counterfactual Response Trajectories for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11238)
*Haoqian Zhang,Ziyuan Yang,Zerui Shao,Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出CADT方法，将扩散模型的数据归因从标量响应重构为“因子诱导的内部响应轨迹归因”，通过构建匹配反事实对、提取逐阶段特征并沿去噪过程积分形成轨迹描述子，再以协方差感知的半正定核对查询与训练表示进行校准，从而计算训练样本归因分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型归因方法通常将因子特定效应压缩为标量响应，难以区分不同的内部变化；而扩散模型的语义因子是在去噪过程中通过不断演化的表示动态逐步显现的，因此需要不仅回答“哪些样本重要”，还要刻画其影响“如何”在生成过程中展开。

Method: CADT在相同噪声状态构建匹配反事实对，以隔离因子特定的表示位移，并将其方向与幅度的演化建模为动态归因签名；对每个训练样本与生成查询提取逐阶段特征并沿去噪过程积分，得到轨迹描述子；在训练集上构建因子特定轨迹描述子库，利用其协方差统计构造协方差感知的半正定核，据此校准查询与训练表示，并对校准后的查询轨迹与各训练轨迹进行比较以产生最终归因分数。

Result: 在多个公开数据集上，CADT在层级归因、组合归因与风格归因三类任务中均持续优于现有扩散归因基线。

Conclusion: 将扩散数据归因重新表述为因子诱导的内部响应轨迹归因是有效且必要的；通过匹配反事实对、动态轨迹描述子与协方差感知核校准，CADT能够更细粒度地捕捉训练样本对生成过程的影响方式，并在多类归因任务上取得一致提升。

Abstract: Diffusion models have achieved remarkable success in image generation, yet tracing their outputs to individual training examples remains challenging. Existing attribution methods often compress factor-specific effects into scalar responses, making distinct internal changes indistinguishable. This is particularly limiting for diffusion models, where semantic factors emerge through evolving representation dynamics during denoising. We therefore reformulate diffusion data attribution as attributing factor-induced internal response trajectories. In this paper, we propose a novel Concept Attribution method through Dynamic Trajectories(CADT). We argue that attribution should therefore ask not only \emph{which} examples matter, but also \emph{how} their influence unfolds during generation. Specifically, we construct matched counterfactual pairs at identical noisy states to isolate factor-specific representation displacements, and model their directional and magnitude evolution across denoising as dynamic attribution signatures. For each training example and generated query, CADT extracts stage-wise feature vectors and integrates them along the denoising process to form a trajectory descriptor. Applying the same construction across the training set yields a bank of factor-specific trajectory descriptors. The covariance statistics of this bank are then used to construct . CADT uses this covariance-aware positive-semidefinite kernel to calibrate the query and training representations, and compares the calibrated query trajectory with each training trajectory to produce the final training-sample attribution scores. Experiments on multiple public datasets show consistent improvements over existing diffusion attribution baselines across hierarchical, compositional, and style attribution.

</details>


### [57] [TAP3D: Thermal-Assisted 3D Human Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11241)
*Xie Zhang,Chengxiao Li,Xuan Liu,Chenshu Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TAP3D系统，首次利用低成本热阵列从人体热特征重建3D人体点云，实现隐私友好、无源的人体感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LiDAR、雷达和深度相机的人体点云方法存在成本高、重建稀疏、隐私担忧等固有缺陷，因此需要一种低成本、高密度、隐私安全的替代方案。

Method: 采用单商用热阵列传感器，提出融合前向热物理模型的新型物理信息设计，包含两个模块：多基元估计（自监督联合恢复深度及其他热属性）与几何透视融合（抑制干扰并分离多人）。

Result: 构建了大规模数据集（16万样本、8个环境、11名用户）。实现了高精度稠密点云生成，支持下游任务：跌倒检测91.46%、室内跟踪MAE 21.86 cm、人体网格恢复误差4.87 cm。

Conclusion: TAP3D首次将人体热量转化为点云，开创了隐私优先、完全无源的人体感知新范式，具有广泛应用前景，并已开源。

Abstract: Human body point clouds are a versatile representation for AI-enabled human sensing. However, existing methods using LiDAR, radar, and depth cameras suffer from inherent drawbacks in high cost, sparse reconstruction, and privacy concerns, etc. In this paper, we exploit low-cost thermal arrays and present TAP3D, the first system to reconstruct 3D human point clouds from body heat signatures, offering significant advantages in cost, density, human sensitivity, and privacy. To overcome major challenges in depth estimation, thermal interference, and multi-person separation, we propose a novel physics-informed design, which integrates a forward thermal physics model with two distinct modules: multi-primitive estimation for self-supervised joint recovery of depth and other thermal properties, and geometric perspective fusion for suppressing interference and disentangling multiple people. We implement TAP3D using a single commodity thermal array sensor and build a large-scale dataset (160K samples, 8 environments, 11 users) for evaluation. TAP3D achieves remarkable accuracy for dense point cloud generation, enabling downstream tasks like fall detection (91.46%), indoor tracking (21.86 cm MAE), and human mesh recovery (4.87 cm error). By transforming body heat into point clouds for the first time, TAP3D pioneers a new paradigm for privacy-first, fully passive human sensing for many applications. TAP3D is open-sourced at https://github.com/aiot-lab/TAP3D.

</details>


### [58] [V-CoLA: Vision Token Compression with Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11251)
*Hao Jiang,Yiru Mao,Tianpeng Bu,Hao Zhou,Hongtao Duan,Wang Jing,Bowen Xu,Xin Chen,Lulu Hu,Bin Yang,Yongliang Tao,Minying Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练视觉Token压缩框架V-CoLA，专为线性注意力混合架构设计，在保留50%视觉Token时达到原性能99.5%，12.5%时仍超过88%，并实现1.86×至6.15×的预填充加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLM）中视觉Token占据输入序列主导地位，带来显著计算开销，故视觉Token压缩成为缓解负担的关键方向。然而，随着引入线性注意力的混合架构（如Qwen3.5）出现，原先面向softmax注意力的压缩方法难以泛化：基于注意力和基于相似度的方法均出现明显性能退化，亟需针对该新范式的压缩方法。

Method: 提出V-CoLA——一种免训练、专为线性注意力设计的视觉Token压缩框架。其核心包括：（1）新颖的"唯一性感知重要性准则"（uniqueness-aware importance criterion），用于识别关键视觉Token；（2）自适应Token合并策略（adaptive token merging strategy）执行压缩。所有组件在实现层面进行优化，以兼容线性注意力的分块并行（chunk-wise parallelism）。

Result: 在多个基准测试上，V-CoLA仅使用50.0%视觉Token即可保持原性能的99.5%，在仅12.5%Token时仍保持88.0%以上性能，同时带来1.86×至6.15×的预填充（prefill）加速。

Conclusion: V-CoLA验证了针对线性注意力架构定制视觉Token压缩的必要性与有效性，在免训练前提下实现了性能与效率的良好权衡，并保持与分块并行的兼容性，具备较强实用价值。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have demonstrated impressive capabilities but suffer from substantial computational overhead, as vision tokens dominate the input sequence. This motivates vision token compression as a key direction to alleviate the burden. However, with the emergence of hybrid architectures incorporating linear attention (\eg, Qwen3.5), prior methods designed for softmax attention struggle to generalize. Our analysis reveals that both attention- and similarity-based approaches suffer notable performance degradation, underscoring the urgent need for compression methods tailored to this regime. To this end, we propose \textbf{V-CoLA}, an efficient training-free token compression framework specifically designed for linear attention. V-CoLA introduces a novel \textit{uniqueness-aware importance criterion} for identifying critical vision tokens, coupled with an \textit{adaptive token merging strategy} that performs compression. All components are optimized at the implementation level to remain compatible with the chunk-wise parallelism of linear attention, ensuring strong practical value. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate the superiority of V-CoLA: it achieves 99.5\% of the original performance with only 50.0\% of vision tokens, and over 88.0\% with as few as 12.5\%, while delivering a 1.86$\times$ to 6.15$\times$ prefill speedup.

</details>


### [59] [LR-V2X: Loss-Resilient Collaborative Perception under Low-Bandwidth Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11264)
*Kang Yang,Tianci Bu,Peng Wang,Deying Li,Yongcai Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LR-V2X框架，将受损潜表示转化为空间先验并借助自车上下文重建缺失BEV信息，实现丢包鲁棒的V2X协同感知，通信开销较稠密BEV融合降低64倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车载无线通信存在丢包不可预测性，V2X协同感知需在丢包、低带宽条件下实现可靠协作。现有稠密BEV特征融合依赖冗余特征交换，低带宽下不可行；紧凑通信方法虽能压缩消息，但丢包后难以恢复缺失特征内容。

Method: LR-V2X为丢包鲁棒的潜空间重建框架：将受损的接收潜表示（即使在90%严重丢包下）转换为空间先验，再以自车上下文为条件从该先验重建缺失BEV信息。模型可仅在完整通信条件下训练，测试时直接应用于有损条件，无需针对大量丢包情形训练。

Result: 在DAIR-V2X和V2XREAL上，LR-V2X在严重丢包下展现最强鲁棒性，随通信质量下降仍保持可靠协作；通信开销较稠密BEV特征融合基线降低64倍。

Conclusion: LR-V2X通过潜空间重建与训练-测试条件解耦，兼顾丢包鲁棒性与低带宽效率，为V2X协同感知提供实用方案。代码将开源。

Abstract: Given the inherent unpredictability of packet loss in vehicular wireless communications, V2X collaborative perception can yield practical benefits only if agents can achieve reliable collaboration under lossy and low-bandwidth communication conditions. Existing dense BEV feature fusion methods depend on redundant BEV feature exchange, which is infeasible in low-bandwidth scenarios, while compact-communication methods aggressively compress messages but can hardly recover the missing feature content after packet loss. In this paper, we present LR-V2X, a loss-resilient, latent-space reconstruction framework that converts corrupted received latents (even under severe 90% packet loss) into a spatial prior and then reconstructs the missing BEV information from this informative prior and using ego context as condition. Notably, the model can be trained under complete communication conditions and can be directly applied to lossy conditions at test time, eliminating the need for training under numerous lossy conditions. Experiments on DAIR-V2X and V2XREAL show that LR-V2X delivers the strongest robustness under severe packet loss and preserves reliable collaboration as communication quality degrades. And it reduces communication overhead by $64\times$ compared to dense BEV feature fusion baselines. Code will be released at https://github.com/sidiangongyuan/LR-V2X.

</details>


### [60] [When Scene Text Hijacks the Scene: Uncovering, Exploiting, and Mitigating Rendered-Text Semantic Leakage in Image Generation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11286)
*Feifei Li,Runjie Wang,Xiaohan Zhang,Zhenxing Qian,Mi Wen,Mi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究图像生成模型（IGM）中的"渲染文本语义泄漏"现象：本应仅作为局部视觉约束、需逐字复现的场景文本，因其语言学语义被模型当作输入指令的一部分解读，构成了新的语义控制通道，可能被用于向非文本区域传递有害语义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代图像生成模型（如 Nano Banana、GPT-Image）已支持复杂指令跟随、逼真图像合成与可控场景文本渲染。随着能力扩展，复杂提示引入了新的控制通道，安全分析必须覆盖这些通道。而渲染文本虽名义上是局部视觉约束，却携带可被模型解释的语义，其安全隐患尚未被充分理解，因此需要系统性地刻画该现象。

Method: 1）将主视觉提示与渲染文本解耦，分别测量二者对生成图像的独立效应与组合效应；2）量化语义泄漏程度与文本渲染保真度；3）分析泄漏如何从模型中间层证据中产生；4）验证嵌入场景文本的有害语义能否穿过基于LLM的提示增强流程，并在主视觉提示无害时仍操控非文本图像区域；5）在 FLUX-2-dev 上提出初步缓解方法。

Result: 实验确认存在显著的渲染文本语义泄漏：渲染文本的语义会独立且可组合地影响生成结果，并可从中间层证据中观察到泄漏的形成过程；有害语义能穿透LLM提示增强管线并操控非文本区域，即便主视觉提示本身无害。所提出的初步缓解方法在 FLUX-2-dev 上能够减少从渲染文本到非文本区域的不安全语义迁移，同时保持预期的文本渲染行为。

Conclusion: 渲染文本是"可见数据+潜在语义"的双用途载体，构成现代IGM中以文本为中心的跨模态攻击面。研究结果提示：安全对齐与内容审核不能只关注主视觉提示，还须覆盖场景文本这一被忽视的语义控制通道。

Abstract: The reliability and accountability of image generative models (IGMs) are essential for building responsible and trustworthy AI systems. Recent IGMs, such as Nano Banana and GPT-Image, now support complex instruction following, realistic image synthesis, and controllable scene-text rendering. As these capabilities expand, safety analysis must also account for new control channels introduced by complex prompts. In this work, we study rendered-text semantic leakage, a largely overlooked phenomenon in open-domain text rendering. Although rendered text is intended to serve as a local visual constraint that should be reproduced verbatim in the generated image, it also carries linguistic semantics that may be interpreted by the model as part of the input instruction. This makes rendered text a potential semantic control channel whose safety implications remain insufficiently understood. We systematically characterize this phenomenon by decoupling the main visual prompt from the rendered text and measuring their individual and compositional effects on generated images. We quantify semantic leakage and rendering fidelity, and further analyze how leakage emerges from intermediate model evidence. We then show that harmful semantics embedded in scene text can persist through LLM-based prompt enhancement pipelines and steer non-text image regions, even when the main visual prompt remains benign. Finally, we propose a preliminary mitigation approach that reduces unsafe semantic transfer from rendered text to non-text regions while preserving the intended text-rendering behavior on FLUX-2-dev. Our findings reveal rendered text as a dual-use carrier of visible data and latent semantics, exposing a text-centric cross-modal attack surface in modern IGMs.

</details>


### [61] [Spatial-Frequency-Aware Implicit Neural Representation of Multidimensional Signals via MLP-KAN Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11296)
*Wen Yan,Ligen Shi,Jun Qiu,Haimiao Zhang,Lina Wu,Chang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出空间-频率感知的INR框架，将MLP分支与KAN分支通过小波域融合，并施加频带分离正则化，在多类信号模态上提升重建保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于MLP的隐式神经表示（INR）存在固有频谱偏置，偏好低频分量、抑制高频细节重建；现有Fourier特征映射等方法依赖敏感的手工调参且易产生频谱伪影。因此需要一种能自动实现频率互补建模的框架。

Method: 构建空间-频率感知INR：MLP分支负责低频平滑结构，KAN分支补充局部变化与精细细节。在输出融合阶段引入离散小波变换（DWT）与逆离散小波变换（IDWT），将两分支输出分解为小波系数，对应系数加性融合后再做逆小波重建。同时提出小波域频带分离正则化，惩罚MLP分支的高频响应与KAN分支的低频响应，促使两分支形成互补的频率取向。

Result: 在1D信号、2D图像、3D体数据与符号距离函数（SDF）、视频以及4D光场等模态上的实验表明，该表示在所评估的信号模态中均适用，并取得重建保真度的提升。

Conclusion: 通过MLP与KAN双分支的频率互补建模结合小波域融合与频带分离正则化，可有效缓解INR的频谱偏置问题，无需敏感手工调参即可在多种信号模态上改善重建质量。

Abstract: Implicit Neural Representations (INRs) have emerged as a compelling paradigm for modeling multidimensional signals by mapping continuous coordinates to signal values. However, Multi-Layer Perceptrons (MLP)-based INRs inherently suffer from spectral bias, which favors low-frequency components and suppresses the reconstruction of essential high-frequency details. While existing techniques, such as Fourier feature mappings, mitigate this issue, they often rely on sensitive manual tuning and are prone to spectral artifacts. In this paper, we propose a spatial-frequency-aware INR framework that combines an MLP branch with a Kolmogorov-Arnold network (KAN) branch for complementary frequency-oriented modeling. The MLP branch provides a low-frequency-oriented representation of smooth structures, whereas the KAN branch complements localized variations and fine details. To coordinate the two branches, we integrate the discrete wavelet transform (DWT) and inverse discrete wavelet transform (IDWT) into the output fusion stage. The outputs of the two branches are decomposed into wavelet coefficients, and the corresponding coefficients are additively fused before inverse wavelet reconstruction. A wavelet-domain band-separation regularization further penalizes high-frequency responses in the MLP branch and low-frequency responses in the KAN branch, thereby encouraging complementary frequency-oriented behavior. Experiments on 1D signals, 2D images, 3D volumes and signed distance functions, videos, and 4D light-fields demonstrate the applicability of the proposed representation across the evaluated signal modalities. Results demonstrate improved reconstruction fidelity across the evaluated signal modalities.

</details>


### [62] [iCATS: Fast Video Generation via Interaction-Aware Sparse Attention and Timestep-Adaptive Sparsity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11302)
*Chengfeng Han,Baole Ai,Xianlu Bian,Jie Yao,Zilong Huang,Ang Wang,Dandan Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: iCATS提出免训练稀疏注意力加速框架，通过改进重要性估计、稀疏掩码构造与硬件执行策略，在DiT上实现更优的效率-质量权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 免训练稀疏注意力通过减少计算来加速Diffusion Transformers，但需估计query-key区域重要性并生成稀疏掩码，仅计算重要候选，这不可避免地引入近似误差并可能降低生成质量。因此需要更好地平衡效率与质量。

Method: iCATS整合三方面改进：(1) 重要性估计：不同于先前基于特征相似度对query和key独立聚类，iCATS采用基于query-key点积交互的聚类，并将其目标重新表述为简单的二次型以实现低成本计算；(2) 稀疏掩码构造：发现不同去噪时间步对稀疏近似误差的容忍度不同，提出SNR引导的稀疏调度动态调整稀疏度；(3) 硬件执行：设计尾部合并策略减少不规则簇大小导致的填充开销，提升GPU内核利用率。

Result: 在HunyuanVideo-T2V-13B上实现2.03倍加速，PSNR为31.017 dB；在Wan2.1-T2V-14B上实现1.55倍加速，PSNR为29.301 dB，达到最先进的效率-质量权衡。

Conclusion: iCATS通过改进的重要性估计、SNR引导的动态稀疏调度及尾部合并硬件优化，在免训练稀疏注意力框架下显著提升DiT推理效率并保持生成质量，取得最佳效率-质量权衡。

Abstract: Training-free sparse attention offers a practical acceleration solution to Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) via reducing computations without fine-tuning. It typically involves estimating the importance of query-key regions and deriving sparse masks to compute only the important candidates, which inevitably introduces approximation errors that may degrade generation quality. To better balance the efficiency-quality trade-off, we propose iCATS, integrating improved importance estimation and sparse mask construction with an efficient hardware execution strategy. Specifically, for importance estimation, unlike previous works that perform independent clustering over query and key tokens based on feature similarity to estimate attention scores, iCATS demonstrates that clustering based on query-key dot-product interactions is more accurate and further reformulates this objective as a simple quadratic form for low-cost computation. For sparse mask construction, instead of using a fixed top-p rule, we observe that tolerance to sparse approximation errors varies across denoising timesteps and therefore introduce an SNR-guided sparsity schedule to adjust sparsity dynamically, leading to higher accuracy. Finally, for hardware execution, we devise a tail-merging strategy to reduce padding overhead caused by irregular cluster sizes, improving GPU kernel utilization. Extensive experiments show that iCATS achieves $2.03\times$ acceleration with 31.017 dB PSNR on HunyuanVideo-T2V-13B and $1.55\times$ acceleration with 29.301 dB PSNR on Wan2.1-T2V-14B, delivering a state-of-the-art efficiency-quality trade-off.

</details>


### [63] [Efficient Multi-Granularity Knowledge Transfer for Radiology Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11303)
*Xubin Zhong,Zheyu Zhang,Wenjian Qin,Ning Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EMGKT方法，通过多粒度知识迁移提升放射报告生成的医学知识表达，可高效嵌入现有方法并验证其有效性与可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射报告生成需医学知识以准确识别疾病并专业描述，但现有方法忽视了增强关键区域医学知识的重要性，而该能力要求模型在多粒度层次上有效提取与聚合知识。

Method: 1) 使用医学视觉-语言模型编码全局知识嵌入以提供上下文医学知识；2) 设计细粒度知识蒸馏(FGKD)训练任务，教师嵌入由额外先验编码，学生嵌入通过蒸馏学习，推理时仅用学生嵌入，成本可忽略且无需额外先验；3) 构建疾病诊断混合专家分类器，用疾病嵌入初始化并建模为不同专家以适配多粒度特征；EMGKT可高效应用于多数现有方法。

Result: 在两个广泛使用的公开数据集及多种基线上进行大量实验，验证了EMGKT的有效性与可迁移性。

Conclusion: EMGKT通过全局知识编码、细粒度知识蒸馏与混合专家分类器实现多粒度医学知识迁移，兼顾性能与效率，并具备良好的通用性与可迁移性。

Abstract: Radiology report generation can automatically generate clinical descriptions from X-ray images, thereby significantly improving the efficiency of radiologists. This task is challenging because it requires medical knowledge to accurately identify diseases and describe them in a professional manner. However, existing methods often overlook the importance of enhancing medical knowledge in describing pivotal areas, a capability that requires models to effectively extract and aggregate knowledge at multiple levels of granularity. Accordingly, we herein propose a novel and compact Efficient Multi-Granularity Knowledge Transfer (\textbf{EMGKT}) method to address the above issues. First, we encode global knowledge embeddings using a medical vision-language model, which provides contextual medical knowledge. Moreover, we devise a novel Fine-Grained Knowledge Distillation (FGKD) training task which efficiently extract fine-grained knowledge. Specifically, the FGKD training task contains teacher embeddings and student embeddings. Teacher embeddings are encoded using extra priors; while student embeddings are learned from the teacher embeddings through knowledge distillation. During inference, the student embeddings are used to enhance fine-grained knowledge while the teacher embeddings are discarded, resulting in negligible computational costs and no need for extra priors. Finally, we further develop a mixture of disease diagnosis expert classifiers to enhance knowledge extraction. The classifiers are initialized using disease embeddings and are modeled as different experts to address various granularity features. Notably, \textbf{EMGKT} can be efficiently applied to most existing methods. Extensive experiments are conducted on two widely-used public datasets and various baselines, which demonstrates the effectiveness and transferability of \textbf{EMGKT}.

</details>


### [64] [Collaboratively Guided Adversarial Robust Distillation with Teacher-Favorable Examples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11306)
*Zhi Li,Haowei Liu,Hongchen Yang,Xiaoxuan Wang,Song Gao,Shaowen Yao,Wei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出协同引导的对抗鲁棒蒸馏方法CGARD，在同一扰动邻域内联合优化学生对抗样本与教师协同样本，以提升鲁棒知识迁移效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗蒸馏主要利用教师对干净样本或对抗样本的预测来监督学生，但忽视了扰动邻域内"对教师有利"的监督信号这一未被充分探索的方向。

Method: CGARD在同一扰动邻域内联合优化两类样本：学生对抗样本（用于对抗教师监督）与教师协同样本（约束其在教师下的交叉熵损失不高于干净输入），将协同教师引导与对抗教师监督相结合。

Result: 在CIFAR-10与CIFAR-100上进行白盒评估及额外的黑盒迁移评估，相比强对抗蒸馏基线取得一致的鲁棒性提升。

Conclusion: 在扰动邻域中引入教师友好的协同引导，可有效补充对抗监督，实现更优的鲁棒知识蒸馏。

Abstract: Adversarial distillation transfers robustness from high-capacity teachers to compact students. Existing adversarial distillation methods mainly use teacher predictions on clean or adversarial examples to supervise student learning. However, teacher-favorable supervision within the perturbation neighborhood remains underexplored in adversarial distillation. We therefore propose Collaboratively Guided Adversarial Robust Distillation (CGARD), which jointly optimizes distinct student-adversarial and teacher-collaborative examples within the same perturbation neighborhood. The teacher-collaborative example is constrained to incur no greater cross-entropy loss under the teacher than the clean input. CGARD combines collaborative teacher guidance with adversarial teacher supervision to improve robust knowledge transfer. Experiments on CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100, including white-box evaluation and additional black-box transfer evaluation, demonstrate consistent robustness improvements over strong adversarial distillation baselines.

</details>


### [65] [FloorSAV: Elucidating Spatial Audio-Visual Context with 2D Floormap for AV-LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11310)
*Kyeong-Rae Kim,Sungnyun Kim,Tae-Hyun Oh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FloorSAV 通过渲染动态 2D 平面地图（floormap）作为同步流注入音视频大模型，无需微调即可提升具身场景下的 3D 空间推理能力，并提出了 SAVED-Bench 基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态自我中心环境中的 3D 空间推理对具身智能至关重要，但音视频大模型（AV-LLMs）缺乏直接从原始感知流中处理和内化全局几何信息的显式机制。现有方法要么需要昂贵的微调，要么未充分利用模型的跨模态推理能力。

Method: 提出 FloorSAV 框架：整合 3D 点云、相机轨迹、空间音频线索和语义化物体地标，渲染出动态 2D 平面地图，并作为与自我中心视频同步的流注入 AV-LLM。模型在单次推理中联合视觉、听觉和几何线索，并辅以平面地图解读引导。同时构建 SAVED-Bench 基准，包含动态相对性、区域推理和路径推理三类空间问答任务。

Result: FloorSAV 在 SAVED-Bench 和 SAVVY-Bench 的多项任务上提升了 AV-LLM 的空间推理性能。使用真实标注平面地图的研究进一步证明了 FloorSAV 在精确空间信息条件下的巨大潜力。

Conclusion: 通过显式渲染平面地图作为同步模态，可在不微调的情况下有效激活 AV-LLM 的跨模态空间推理能力，为具身空间智能提供了新思路。

Abstract: While 3D spatial reasoning in dynamic egocentric environments is crucial for embodied intelligence, audio-visual large language models (AV-LLMs) lack explicit mechanisms to process and internalize global geometry directly from raw sensory streams. Existing approaches either require costly fine-tuning or underutilize the model's cross-modal reasoning capacities. In this paper, we propose FloorSAV, a novel framework that explicitly grounds spatial audio-visual context by rendering a dynamic 2D floormap. By integrating 3D point clouds, camera trajectories, spatial audio cues, and semantically grounded object landmarks, we inject this floormap into the AV-LLM as a synchronized stream with an egocentric video. AV-LLMs utilize their multi-modal capabilities to jointly reason over visual, auditory, and geometric cues in a single inference with floormap interpretation guidance. We further introduce SAVED-Bench (Spatial Audio-Visual Egocentric Benchmark with Dynamic Agents), constructing essential tasks of spatial capability in real-world scenarios: dynamic relativity, regional, and path reasoning QAs. FloorSAV improves AV-LLMs' spatial reasoning on various tasks from both SAVED-Bench and SAVVY-Bench. Studies with ground-truth floormaps demonstrate the substantial potential of FloorSAV with accurate spatial information.

</details>


### [66] [It's Always 10:10: Reference Images Break a Bias That Prompts Only Dent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11320)
*Luca Cazzaniga*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究测量了文生图模型对"10:10"钟表显示的偏好偏差，并在52个模型上测试了三种克服方法。结果显示，不指定时间时67%的图像三个钟表均显示10:10；提供数字时间可降低偏差，而附上参考表盘的方法将全对率提升至75%，几乎消除全为10:10的图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文生图模型会复现训练图像中的习惯性模式，其中模拟时钟是极端案例：广告中的手表几乎总显示10:10，导致生成模型即使被要求其他时间也会回到10:10。研究旨在量化这一偏差并寻找有效的克服方法。

Method: 在Magnific平台的52个模型上测试，并在Higgsfield上复制。每张图像要求显示三个相同钟表，时间分别为2:35、6:50和11:20。固定物体描述，仅改变时间请求方式：(A)不指定时间；(B)数字形式时间；(C)用文字描述指针相对于表盘数字的位置；(D)相同描述加绘制的参考表盘。两名AI读者盲读全部1,799张图像，第三名读者与作者解决分歧（表盘级一致率96.0%和97.3%）。

Result: 不指定时间时，67%的图像三个钟表均为10:10。在20个当前模型上，全对率：数字形式(B)34%，文字描述指针(C)30%，参考表盘(D)75%（D-B: +37个百分点，95% CI +28至+45）；无证据表明文字描述优于数字（C-B: -4个百分点，CI -10至+1）。两平台共享的12个模型复制结果一致（B 54%，C 50%，D 81%）。

Conclusion: 写出时间可减少偏差，但20个当前模型中仍有三分之二的图像至少有一个钟表错误；加入绘制的参考表盘将全对率提升至四分之三，并几乎消除全部为10:10的图像。作者发布了所有图像、提示词、原始读数及可重算所有结果的脚本。

Abstract: Text-to-image models appear to reproduce the habits of the photographs they learned from. Analog clocks are an extreme case: in advertising, watches almost always show 10:10, and generated clocks return to 10:10 even when another time is requested. We measure this bias and test three ways of overcoming it on 52 models available on the Magnific platform, with a replication on Higgsfield. Every image shows three identical clocks that must show 2:35, 6:50 and 11:20. The description of the object is fixed and only the request about the time changes: no time (A), the time in digits (B), the hand positions described by construction relative to the dial numerals (C), or the same description plus a drawn reference dial (D). Two AI readers read all 1,799 images blind from coded copies, with a third reader and the author settling disagreements (dial-level agreement 96.0% and 97.3%). With no time requested, 67% of the images have all three clocks at 10:10. On the 20 current models, all three clocks are correct in 34% of the images with digits, 30% with the hands described in words and 75% with the reference dial (D-B: +37 points, 95% CI +28 to +45); we found no evidence that describing the hands in words beats the digits (C-B: -4 points, CI -10 to +1). The replication on the 12 models shared by both platforms gives the same picture (B 54%, C 50%, D 81%). Writing the time reduces the bias but leaves two thirds of the images of the 20 current models with at least one wrong clock; adding a drawn reference raises full accuracy to three quarters and almost eliminates images entirely at 10:10. We release all images, prompts, raw readings and a script that recomputes every result.

</details>


### [67] [PathLang: A Language-Centered Benchmark for Vision-Language Models in Computational Pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11329)
*Fanqi Cheng,Kuo Gong,Shangke Liu,Beidi Zhao,Junchao Zhu,Zheyu Zhu,Leiyue Zhao,Fengbei Liu,John Cannon,Gang Wang,Zu-hua Gao,Kenji Ikemura,Yihe Yang,Yaohong Wang,Yuankai Huo,Xiaoxiao Li,Mert R. Sabuncu,Ruining Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出病理语言中心零样本基准 PathLang，固定图像与标签、仅系统变换诊断语言，经六位病理医师验证，覆盖四类任务，在九模型五数据集上揭示性能对临床等价改写高度敏感、类间可分性与图文对齐质量不必然一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病理视觉-语言模型（VLM）的视觉感知能力已较强，但其语言域鲁棒性研究不足。现有基准多依赖规范闭集提示，或仅扰动通用模板，将语言视为固定的评价组件而非可变轴；而临床实践中诊断表述随报告、机构与候选诊断而变化，亟需以语言为中心的临床化评测。

Method: 构建语言中心、临床化的零样本基准 PathLang：保持切片、真值标签与图文评测方向不变，仅系统变换诊断语言，使性能差异反映表述方式而非图像内容。语言变化遵循病理医师实际改写诊断的方式（术语、特异性、报告风格），提示与候选池均由六位执业病理医师验证。基准涵盖四类任务族：(1) 零样本分类与图文对齐分析；(2) 跨模态检索；(3) 复述鲁棒性，含语义等价复述、长度与报告风格变化及提示集成；(4) 在四类具有不同语义竞争形式的候选池上进行开放词表诊断检索。

Result: 在跨四个器官的五个公开数据集与九个 VLM 上：性能对临床等价复述高度敏感；在不同语义竞争形式间差异显著；图文对齐质量不一定转化为类间可分性。

Conclusion: 语言表述是病理 VLM 行为的独立敏感变量，仅凭图文对齐分数不足以评估诊断判别能力。作者开源提示语料、候选池、预计算文本嵌入与评测代码，为病理 VLM 的语言鲁棒性提供标准化评测基础。

Abstract: Pathology vision-language models (VLMs) have shown strong visual perception ability, but their robustness in the language domain remains poorly characterized. Existing pathology VLM benchmarks largely rely on canonical closed-set prompts or perturb only generic templates, treating language as a fixed evaluation component rather than a variable axis of model behavior. In clinical practice, however, diagnostic language varies across reports, institutions, and candidate diagnoses. We introduce PathLang, a language-centered and clinically grounded zero-shot benchmark. PathLang holds the underlying slides, ground-truth labels, and image-text evaluation direction fixed while systematically varying only the diagnostic language, so that performance differences reflect how a diagnosis is phrased rather than what is imaged. The language variation follows how pathologists actually rephrase diagnoses (terminology, specificity, and reporting style), and all prompts and candidate pools are validated by six board-certified pathologists. PathLang covers four task families: (1) zero-shot classification with image-text alignment analysis, (2) cross-modal retrieval, (3) paraphrase robustness, including semantic-equivalence paraphrases, length and reporting-style variation, and prompt ensembling, and (4) open-vocabulary diagnosis retrieval over four candidate pools with distinct forms of semantic competition. Across nine VLMs and five public datasets spanning four organs, we find that performance is highly sensitive to clinically equivalent paraphrases, varies substantially across forms of semantic competition, and that image-text alignment quality does not necessarily translate into inter-class separability. We release the prompt corpus, candidate pools, pre-computed text embeddings, and evaluation code at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/PathLang.

</details>


### [68] [Point-Focused Attention Meets Context-Scan State Space: Robust Biological Visual Perception for Point Cloud Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11342)
*Kanglin Qu,Pan Gao,Qun Dai,Yuanhao Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PointLearner，一种仿生点云表征学习网络，通过"聚焦-后上下文"策略同时建模局部几何结构与长程依赖：用点聚焦注意力模拟中央凹视觉，用基于Hilbert曲线的上下文扫描状态空间模拟眼动扫视推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点云表征学习中，如何协同捕获复杂的局部结构与全局上下文依赖是一个关键挑战。传统方法难以兼顾局部几何建模与长程依赖交互，作者希望借助生物视觉的主动式、中央凹启发的处理策略来同时解决这两方面问题。

Method: 1) 点聚焦注意力（point-focused attention）：在视觉焦点处模拟中央凹视觉，通过局部邻域与空间下采样特征之间的竞争归一化注意力机制实现；其中空间下采样特征由基于可学习诱导点（learnable inducing points）的池化方法提取，诱导点数量可控且直接与点云交互，从而灵活适应点云的非均匀分布。2) 上下文扫描状态空间（context-scan state space）：模拟眼睛的扫视推断，通过由Hilbert曲线引导的双向S6扫描路径推断场景的整体语义结构与空间内容。二者结合形成"先聚焦、后上下文"的仿生设计。

Result: PointLearner展现出显著的鲁棒性，并在多个点云任务上取得了最先进（state-of-the-art）的性能。

Conclusion: 通过将中央凹启发的点聚焦注意力与扫视启发的上下文扫描状态空间相结合，"聚焦-后上下文"的仿生架构能够有效协同局部几何建模与长程依赖交互，为点云表征学习提供了一种高效且鲁棒的新范式。

Abstract: Synergistically capturing intricate local structures and global contextual dependencies has become a critical challenge in point cloud representation learning. To address this, we introduce PointLearner, a point cloud representation learning network that closely aligns with biological vision which employs an active, foveation-inspired processing strategy, thus enabling local geometric modeling and long-range dependency interactions simultaneously. Specifically, we first design a point-focused attention, which simulates foveal vision at the visual focus through a competitive normalized attention mechanism between local neighbors and spatially downsampled features. The spatially downsampled features are extracted by a pooling method based on learnable inducing points, which can flexibly adapt to the non-uniform distribution of point clouds as the number of inducing points is controlled and they interact directly with point clouds. Second, we propose a context-scan state space that mimics eye's saccade inference, which infers the overall semantic structure and spatial content in the scene through a scan path guided by the Hilbert curve for the bidirectional S6.
